def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates insurance premiums."""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        insurance_master = InsuranceMasterC2(INS_LIFE, INS_HEALTH, INS_AUTO, INS_HOME, INS_UMBRELLA, INS_COVERAGE_AMOUNT, INS_CLAIMS_COUNT, INS_PREMIUM_AMOUNT)
        try:
            insurance_master = read_insurance_master_C2()
            WS_EOF = False
            determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_master)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_master)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_master)
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

def read_insurance_master_C2() -> InsuranceMasterC2:
    """Placeholder for reading insurance master data."""
    raise EOFError

def determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_master: InsuranceMasterC2) -> None:
    """Determines the base premium based on insurance type."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_master.ins_life:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000
    elif insurance_master.ins_health:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif insurance_master.ins_auto:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif insurance_master.ins_home:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
    elif insurance_master.ins_umbrella:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE

def apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_master: InsuranceMasterC2) -> None:
    """Applies a risk factor if claims count exceeds 2."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_master.ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal("1.25")

def calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_master: InsuranceMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates and updates the final premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    insurance_master.ins_premium_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates portfolio values."""
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_master = InvestmentMasterC2(INV_QUANTITY, INV_CURRENT_PRICE, INV_PURCHASE_PRICE, INV_MARKET_VALUE, INV_GAIN_LOSS, INV_DIVIDEND_RATE, False, False, False)
        try:
            investment_master = read_investment_master_C2()
            WS_EOF = False
            calculate_position_value_C2(investment_master)
            calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_master)
            update_totals_C2(investment_master)
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

def read_investment_master_C2() -> InvestmentMasterC2:
    """Placeholder for reading investment master data."""
    raise EOFError

def calculate_position_value_C2(investment_master: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates the position value."""
    investment_master.inv_market_value = investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_current_price

def calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_master: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates the gain or loss."""
    investment_master.inv_gain_loss = investment_master.inv_market_value - (investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_purchase_price)

def update_totals_C2(investment_master: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Updates total investments."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_master.inv_market_value

def calculate_dividends_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates dividends."""
    print("CALCULATING DIVIDENDS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_master = InvestmentMasterC2(INV_QUANTITY, INV_CURRENT_PRICE, INV_PURCHASE_PRICE, INV_MARKET_VALUE, INV_GAIN_LOSS, INV_DIVIDEND_RATE, False, False, False)
        try:
            investment_master = read_investment_master_dividends_C2()
            WS_EOF = False
            if investment_master.inv_dividend_rate > 0:
                compute_dividend_C2(investment_master)
                post_dividend_C2()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

def read_investment_master_dividends_C2() -> InvestmentMasterC2:
    """Placeholder for reading investment master data for dividends."""
    raise EOFError

def compute_dividend_C2(investment_master: InvestmentMasterC2) -> None:
    """Computes the dividend amount."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_CALC_AMOUNT = investment_master.inv_market_value * investment_master.inv_dividend_rate / 4

def post_dividend_C2() -> None:
    """Posts the dividend to the total."""

    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS)
    REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL DEPOSITS: " + WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    write_report_line_C2(REPORT_LINE)
    
    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS)
    REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL WITHDRAWALS: " + WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    write_report_line_C2(REPORT_LINE)
    
    WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT = str(WS_TOTAL_LOANS)
    REPORT_LINE = "TOTAL LOANS: " + WS_FORMATTED_AMOUNT
    write_report_line_C2(REPORT_LINE)

def write_transaction_C2() -> None:
    """Writes a transaction record."""
    global TRAN_TIMESTAMP, TRAN_TYPE, TRAN_AMOUNT, TRAN_STATUS, WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    TRAN_TIMESTAMP = WS_CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
    TRAN_TYPE = 'DEP'
    TRAN_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    TRAN_STATUS = 'C'
    transaction_record = TransactionRecordC2(TRAN_TIMESTAMP, TRAN_TYPE, TRAN_AMOUNT, TRAN_STATUS)
    write_transaction_record_C2(transaction_record)

WS_CALC_TAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_1_MAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_2_MAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_3_MAX: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_1_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_2_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_3_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
WS_BRACKET_5_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates tax based on brackets."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, WS_CALC_TAX, WS_BRACKET_1_MAX, WS_BRACKET_2_MAX, WS_BRACKET_3_MAX, WS_BRACKET_1_RATE, WS_BRACKET_2_RATE, WS_BRACKET_3_RATE, WS_BRACKET_5_RATE
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

WS_PROCESS_COUNT: int = 0

def analyze_patterns_C2() -> None:
    """Analyzes transaction patterns."""
    print("ANALYZING TRANSACTION PATTERNS...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        transaction_log = TransactionRecordC2(TRAN_TIMESTAMP, TRAN_TYPE, TRAN_AMOUNT, TRAN_STATUS)
        try:
            transaction_log = read_transaction_log_C2()
            WS_EOF = False
            check_amount_threshold_C2(transaction_log)
            check_frequency_C2()
            check_time_pattern_C2()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

def read_transaction_log_C2() -> TransactionRecordC2:
    """Placeholder for reading transaction log data."""
    raise EOFError

def check_amount_threshold_C2(transaction_log: TransactionRecordC2) -> None:
    """Checks if transaction amount exceeds a threshold."""
    if transaction_log.tran_amount > 10000:
        flag_large_transaction_C2()

def flag_large_transaction_C2() -> None:
    """Flags a large transaction and writes an audit record."""
    global WS_PROCESS_COUNT
    WS_PROCESS_COUNT += 1
    write_audit_C2()

CUST_CREDIT_SCORE: int = 0
CUST_TOTAL_LOANS: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
CUST_RISK_RATING: str = ""

@dataclass
class CustomerMasterC2:
    """Represents customer master data."""
    cust_credit_score: int
    cust_total_loans: Decimal
    cust_total_balance: Decimal
    cust_risk_rating: str

WS_CALC_RESULT: int = 0
WS_TEMP_FLAG: str = ""

def behavioral_scoring_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates behavioral scores for customers."""
    print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        customer_master = CustomerMasterC2(CUST_CREDIT_SCORE, CUST_TOTAL_LOANS, CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE, CUST_RISK_RATING)
        try:
            customer_master = read_customer_master_C2()
            WS_EOF = False
            calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_master)
            update_customer_profile_C2(customer_master)
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

def read_customer_master_C2() -> CustomerMasterC2:
    """Placeholder for reading customer master data."""
    raise EOFError

def calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_master: CustomerMasterC2) -> None:
    """Calculates a risk score based on credit score and loan balance."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = 0
    if customer_master.cust_credit_score < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if customer_master.cust_total_loans > customer_master.cust_total_balance:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20

def update_customer_profile_C2(customer_master: CustomerMasterC2) -> None:
    """Updates customer profile with risk rating based on calculated score."""
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'M'
    else:
        customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'L'

def aml_screening_C2() -> None:
    """Performs AML screening."""
    print("PERFORMING AML SCREENING...")
    global WS_NOT_EOF, WS_EOF
    WS_NOT_EOF = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        transaction_log = TransactionRecordC2(TRAN_TIMESTAMP, TRAN_TYPE, TRAN_AMOUNT, TRAN_STATUS)
        try:
            transaction_log = read_transaction_log_aml_C2()
            WS_EOF = False
            if transaction_log.tran_amount >= 10000:
                ctr_filing_C2()
            structuring_check_C2()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF = True

    """Placeholder for reading transaction log data for AML screening."""
    raise EOFError

def check_credit_limit_C2() -> None:
    """Checks if the transaction amount exceeds the overdraft limit."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT, ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT, WS_NOT_APPROVED, WS_APPROVED
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT > ACCT_OVERDRAFT_LIMIT:
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True
    else:
        WS_APPROVED = True

    """Checks the fraud score."""
    pass

        write_transaction_C2()

def calculate_rewards_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates rewards points for a transaction."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, TRAN_AMOUNT, WS_TOTAL_FEES
    WS_CALC_RESULT = TRAN_AMOUNT * Decimal("0.01")
    WS_TOTAL_FEES += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_RESULT

def apply_interest_C2() -> None:
    """Applies interest to the credit card balance."""
    global ACCT_BALANCE, WS_CREDIT_CARD_RATE, WS_CALC_INTEREST
    print("APPLYING CREDIT CARD INTEREST...")
    WS_CALC_INTEREST = ACCT_BALANCE * WS_CREDIT_CARD_RATE / 12
    ACCT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_INTEREST

def dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates debt-to-income ratio."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT, CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT / (CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal("0.43"):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True

def ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates loan-to-value ratio."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE, LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE, WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO = LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE / LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE
    if LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal("0.80"):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    global ws_not_eof
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        read_customer_master_next_C3()

def read_customer_master_next_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_eof
    try:
        customer = next(customer_master_iterator)
        calculate_clv_C3()
        assign_segment_C3()
    except StopIteration:
        ws_eof = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result
    ws_calc_result = (cust_total_balance * ws_savings_rate) + \
                      (cust_total_loans * ws_personal_rate) + \
                      (cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_temp_code
    if ws_calc_result > 10000:
        ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
    elif ws_calc_result > 5000:
        ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
    elif ws_calc_result > 1000:
        ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
    else:
        ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

def default_prediction_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""

    if loan_delinquent:
        ws_calc_result += 25
    if cust_credit_score < 600:
        ws_calc_result += 30

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_amount, ws_total_investments
    if acct_balance > acct_min_balance:
        ws_calc_amount = acct_balance - acct_min_balance
        acct_balance -= ws_calc_amount
        ws_total_investments += ws_calc_amount

    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        read_customer_master_next_A110_C3()

    """Brief description."""
    global ws_eof, ws_process_count
    try:
        customer = next(customer_master_iterator)
        ws_process_count += 1
    except StopIteration:
        ws_eof = True

def completeness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count
    if cust_id == "":
        ws_error_count += 1

def accuracy_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count
    if cust_credit_score < 300 or cust_credit_score > 850:
        ws_error_count += 1

def consistency_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    continue_C3()

def timeliness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count
    if cust_last_activity < ws_current_date - 365:
        pass


@dataclass
class Customer:
    cust_id: str
    cust_name: str
    cust_state: str
    cust_credit_score: int
    cust_last_activity: int
    cust_total_balance: Decimal
    cust_total_loans: Decimal
    cust_total_investments: Decimal

# Dummy data for execution
ws_annual_fee_card: Decimal = Decimal('100.00')
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal('100000.00')
ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal('50000.00')
ws_savings_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.02')
ws_personal_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.05')
cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal('5000.00')
cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal('2000.00')
cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal('10000.00')
ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_wire_fee_domestic: Decimal = Decimal('25.00')
ws_wire_fee_intl: Decimal = Decimal('50.00')
loan_delinquent: bool = True
cust_credit_score: int = 550
ws_error_count: int = 0
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_current_date: int = 20240101
cust_id: str = "12345"
cust_name: str = "John Doe"
cust_state: str = "CA"
cust_last_activity: int = 20230101
acct_balance: Decimal = Decimal('15000.00')
acct_min_balance: Decimal = Decimal('10000.00')
WS_SAVINGS_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.05")
WS_PERSONAL_RATE: Decimal = Decimal("0.10")
ACCT_MIN_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("500.00")
WS_NOT_EOF: bool = False
WS_EOF: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_not_approved: bool = False
customer_master_data = [
    Customer("1", "Alice", "NY", 700, 20230101, Decimal("10000.00"), Decimal("5000.00"), Decimal("2000.00")),
    Customer("2", "Bob", "CA", 650, 20230201, Decimal("5000.00"), Decimal("2000.00"), Decimal("1000.00")),
    Customer("3", "Charlie", "TX", 800, 20230301, Decimal("20000.00"), Decimal("10000.00"), Decimal("5000.00")),
]
customer_master_iterator = iter(customer_master_data)

@dataclass
class DataItemC4:
    """Data item representation."""
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_code: Optional[str] = None
    ws_calc_result: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_deposits: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_calc_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_not_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_eof: Optional[bool] = None
    transaction_log: Optional[str] = None
    tran_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_process_count: Optional[int] = 0
    ws_error_count: Optional[int] = 0
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    ws_current_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_string: Optional[str] = None
    ws_valid: Optional[bool] = None
    loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_paid_off: Optional[bool] = None
    ws_atm_fee_foreign: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_formatted_count: Optional[str] = None
    ws_cust_count: Optional[int] = None

def b110_capital_ratios_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Calculating capital ratios."""
    if data_item.ws_total_deposits is not None:
        data_item.ws_calc_result = data_item.ws_total_deposits * Decimal('0.08')

def b120_leverage_ratio_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Calculating leverage ratio."""
    if data_item.ws_total_deposits is not None and data_item.ws_total_loans is not None:
        data_item.ws_calc_result = data_item.ws_total_deposits / data_item.ws_total_loans

    pass

def b410_expected_loss_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Calculating expected loss."""
    if data_item.ws_total_loans is not None:
        data_item.ws_calc_amount = data_item.ws_total_loans * Decimal('0.025')

def b420_allowance_calculation_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Calculating allowance."""
    if data_item.ws_calc_amount is not None and data_item.ws_total_fees is not None:
        data_item.ws_total_fees += data_item.ws_calc_amount

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Monitoring transactions."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    data_item.ws_not_eof = True
    while not data_item.ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading transaction log
        transaction_log_entry = "simulated transaction" # Replace with actual read logic

        if transaction_log_entry:
            c110_rule_based_detection_C4(data_item)
            c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
            c130_network_analysis_C4()
        else:
            data_item.ws_eof = True

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if data_item.tran_amount is not None and data_item.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4(data_item)
    if data_item.tran_amount is not None and 5000 <= data_item.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4(data_item)

def c111_flag_ctr_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    if data_item.ws_process_count is not None:
        data_item.ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Check structuring."""
    if data_item.ws_error_count is not None:
        data_item.ws_error_count += 1

def d110_classification_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if data_item.cust_credit_score is not None:
        if data_item.cust_credit_score > 750:
            data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
        elif data_item.cust_credit_score > 650:
            data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
        elif data_item.cust_credit_score > 550:
            data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
        else:
            data_item.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4(data_item: DataItemC4) -> None:
    """Regression."""
    if data_item.cust_credit_score is not None and data_item.cust_total_balance is not None and data_item.cust_total_loans is not None:
        data_item.ws_calc_result = (data_item.cust_credit_score * 10) + (data_item.cust_total_balance / 1000) - (data_item.cust_total_loans / 2000)

def main_logic_C5() -> None:
    """Main processing loop."""
    ws_not_eof_C5 = True
    while not ws_eof_C5:
        read_customer_master_C5()
        if ws_eof_C5:
            ws_eof_C5 = True
        else:
            i110_update_profile_C5()
            i120_enrich_profile_C5()
            ws_cust_count_C5 += 1

def i110_update_profile_C5() -> None:
    """Update customer profile."""
    cust_last_activity_C5 = ws_current_date_C5

def main_control_0000_C5() -> None:
    """Main control paragraph."""
    initialization_1000_C5()
    while ws_eof_flag_C5 != 'Y':

def initialization_1000_C5() -> None:
    """Initialization paragraph."""
    initialize_ws_work_areas_C5()
    initialize_ws_counters_C5()
    initialize_ws_totals_C5()

    rpt_day_C5 = ws_curr_day_C5

def open_files_1100_C5() -> None:
    """Open input and output files."""
    try:
        customer_file_C5 = open("customer_file", "r") # Replace with actual file opening
        account_file_C5 = open("account_file", "r")
        transaction_file_C5 = open("transaction_file", "r")
        report_file_C5 = open("report_file", "w")
        error_file_C5 = open("error_file", "w")
        master_file_C5 = open("master_file", "r+") # Replace with actual file opening
        ws_file_status_C5 = '00'
    except Exception as e:
        ws_file_status_C5 = '99'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'FILE OPEN ERROR'
        abort_process_9500_C5()
        return # added to exit function after calling abort

def read_parameters_1200_C5() -> None:
    """Read parameters."""
    ws_param_date_C5 = input("Enter date (YYYYMMDD): ")
    ws_param_time_C5 = input("Enter time (HHMMSS): ")
    ws_job_id_C5 = 'batch_001'
    ws_env_type_C5 = 'PRODUCTION'
    ws_process_date_C5 = integer_of_date_function_C5(ws_param_date_C5) # Assuming this function exists

def initialize_tables_1300_C5() -> None:
    """Initialize tables."""
    for ws_tbl_idx_C5 in range(1, 101):
        rate_table_entry_C5 = RateTableEntryC5()  # Assuming this is a data class
        rt_rate_C5 = 0
        rt_code_C5 = ' ' * 10  # Assuming rt_code is PIC X(10)
    for ws_tbl_idx_C5 in range(1, 51):
        branch_table_entry_C5 = BranchTableEntryC5()  # Assuming this is a data class

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """Load reference data."""
    ws_tbl_idx_C5 = 1
    ws_eof_flag_C5 = 'N' # Reset the EOF flag from possible earlier errors
    while ws_eof_flag_C5 != 'Y' and ws_tbl_idx_C5 <= 100:
        try:
            ws_ref_record_C5 = read_reference_file_C5()
            if ws_ref_record_C5 is None:  # Simulate AT END condition
                ws_eof_flag_C5 = 'Y'
            else:
                rt_code_C5 = ws_ref_code_C5
                rt_rate_C5 = ws_ref_rate_C5
                ws_tbl_idx_C5 += 1
        except Exception as e: #Catch read errors, and set to Y
            ws_eof_flag_C5 = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag_C5 = 'N' # Reset for future use
def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """Process transactions."""
    try:
        ws_transaction_rec_C5 = read_transaction_file_C5()
        if ws_transaction_rec_C5 is None:
            ws_eof_flag_C5 = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_trans_count_C5 += 1
            validate_transaction_2100_C5()
            if ws_valid_flag_C5 == 'Y':
                process_by_type_2200_C5()
            else:
                handle_error_2900_C5()
    except Exception as e:
        ws_eof_flag_C5 = 'Y'
        #Handle read errors
def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """Validate a transaction."""
    ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'Y'
    if txn_account_id_C5 == ' ' or txn_account_id_C5 is None: #Use None for low_values
        ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return #EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if not isinstance(txn_amount_C5, (int, float)):
        ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return #EXIT PARAGRAPH
    if txn_type_C5 not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'

    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5() -> None:
    """Validate that the account exists."""
    ws_search_key_C5 = txn_account_id_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag_C5 == 'N':
        ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """Validate business rules."""
    if txn_type_C5 == 'W':
        if txn_amount_C5 > ws_account_balance_C5:
            ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
            ws_error_msg_C5 = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if txn_amount_C5 > 1000000:
        ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

def process_by_type_2200_C5() -> None:
    """Process transaction by type."""
    if txn_type_C5 == 'D':
        process_deposit_2300_C5()
    elif txn_type_C5 == 'W':
        process_withdrawal_2400_C5()
    elif txn_type_C5 == 'T':
        process_transfer_2500_C5()
    elif txn_type_C5 == 'I':
        process_interest_2600_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def process_deposit_2300_C5() -> None:
    """Process a deposit."""
    ws_account_balance_C5 += txn_amount_C5
    ws_txn_desc_C5 = 'DEPOSIT'
    ws_total_deposits_C5 += txn_amount_C5
    ws_deposit_count_C5 += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def update_account_2350_C5() -> None:
    """Update the account record."""
    account_record_C5 = AccountRecordC5()
    account_record_C5.ACCT_BALANCE = ws_account_balance_C5
    account_record_C5.ACCT_LAST_UPDATE = current_date_function_C5()
    try:
        rewrite_account_record_C5(account_record_C5)
        ws_file_status_C5 = '00'
    except:
        ws_file_status_C5 = '99'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'UPDATE FAILED'
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def write_audit_trail_2380_C5() -> None:
    """Write to the audit trail."""
    ws_audit_record_C5 = WsAuditRecordC5()
    ws_audit_record_C5.AUDIT_ACCOUNT = txn_account_id_C5
    ws_audit_record_C5.AUDIT_AMOUNT = txn_amount_C5
    ws_audit_record_C5.AUDIT_TYPE = txn_type_C5
    ws_audit_record_C5.AUDIT_TIMESTAMP = current_date_function_C5()
    ws_audit_record_C5.AUDIT_JOB_ID = ws_job_id_C5
    write_audit_record_C5(ws_audit_record_C5)

def process_withdrawal_2400_C5() -> None:
    """Process a withdrawal."""
    ws_account_balance_C5 -= txn_amount_C5
    ws_txn_desc_C5 = 'WITHDRAWAL'
    ws_total_withdrawals_C5 += txn_amount_C5
    ws_withdrawal_count_C5 += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()
    if ws_account_balance_C5 < ws_min_balance_limit_C5:
        generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5()

def generate_low_balance_alert_2450_C5() -> None:
    """Generate a low balance alert."""
    ws_alert_record_C5 = WsAlertRecordC5()
    ws_alert_record_C5.ALERT_TYPE = 'low_bal'
    ws_alert_record_C5.ALERT_ACCOUNT = txn_account_id_C5
    ws_alert_record_C5.ALERT_BALANCE = ws_account_balance_C5
    ws_alert_record_C5.ALERT_DATE = current_date_function_C5()
    write_alert_record_C5(ws_alert_record_C5)
    ws_alert_count_C5 += 1

def process_transfer_2500_C5() -> None:
    """Process a transfer."""
    validate_target_account_2510_C5()
    if ws_valid_flag_C5 == 'Y':
        debit_source_2520_C5()
        credit_target_2530_C5()
        record_transfer_2540_C5()
    else:
        handle_error_2900_C5()

def validate_target_account_2510_C5() -> None:
    """Validate the target account."""
    ws_search_key_C5 = txn_target_account_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag_C5 == 'N':
        ws_valid_flag_C5 = 'N'
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'TARGET ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def debit_source_2520_C5() -> None:
    """Debit the source account."""
    ws_source_balance_C5 -= txn_amount_C5
    account_record_C5 = AccountRecordC5()
    account_record_C5.ACCT_BALANCE = ws_source_balance_C5
    rewrite_account_record_C5(account_record_C5)

def credit_target_2530_C5() -> None:
    """Credit the target account."""
    ws_target_balance_C5 += txn_amount_C5
    account_record_C5 = AccountRecordC5()
    account_record_C5.ACCT_ID = txn_target_account_C5
    #Read Master File is not required, since this process only adds to the balance
    account_record_C5.ACCT_BALANCE = ws_target_balance_C5
    rewrite_account_record_C5(account_record_C5)

def record_transfer_2540_C5() -> None:
    """Record the transfer."""
    ws_total_transfers_C5 += txn_amount_C5
    ws_transfer_count_C5 += 1
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def process_interest_2600_C5() -> None:
    """Process interest."""
    ws_interest_amount_C5 = ws_account_balance_C5 * ws_interest_rate_C5 / 100
    ws_account_balance_C5 += ws_interest_amount_C5
    ws_txn_desc_C5 = 'INTEREST'
    ws_total_interest_C5 += ws_interest_amount_C5
    ws_interest_count_C5 += 1
    update_account_2350_C5()
    write_audit_trail_2380_C5()

def handle_error_2900_C5() -> None:
    """Handle an error."""
    ws_error_count_C5 += 1
    ws_error_record_C5 = WsErrorRecordC5()
    ws_error_record_C5.ERR_ACCOUNT = txn_account_id_C5
    ws_error_record_C5.ERR_MESSAGE = ws_error_msg_C5
    ws_error_record_C5.ERR_TIMESTAMP = current_date_function_C5()
    write_error_record_C5(ws_error_record_C5)
    if ws_error_count_C5 > ws_max_errors_C5:
        ws_abort_reason_C5 = 'MAX ERRORS EXCEEDED'
        abort_process_9500_C5()

def batch_processing_3000_C5() -> None:
    """Process a batch."""
    load_batch_header_3100_C5()
    while ws_batch_eof_C5 != 'Y':
        process_batch_items_3200_C5()
    validate_batch_totals_3300_C5()
    commit_batch_3400_C5()

def load_batch_header_3100_C5() -> None:
    """Load the batch header."""
    try:
        ws_batch_header_C5 = read_batch_file_C5()
        if ws_batch_header_C5 is None:
            ws_batch_eof_C5 = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_current_batch_C5 = batch_id_C5
            ws_expected_count_C5 = batch_count_C5
            ws_expected_total_C5 = batch_total_C5
    except:
        ws_batch_eof_C5 = 'Y'

def process_batch_items_3200_C5() -> None:
    """Process batch items."""
    try:
        ws_batch_item_C5 = read_batch_file_C5() #Read same file again
        if ws_batch_item_C5 is None:
            ws_batch_eof_C5 = 'Y'
        else:
            ws_actual_count_C5 += 1
            ws_actual_total_C5 += item_amount_C5
            process_single_item_3250_C5()
    except:
        ws_batch_eof_C5 = 'Y'

def process_single_item_3250_C5() -> None:
    """Process a single item."""
    if item_type_C5 == 'PAY':
        process_payment_3260_C5()
    elif item_type_C5 == 'REF':
        process_refund_3270_C5()
    elif item_type_C5 == 'ADJ':
        process_adjustment_3280_C5()

def process_payment_3260_C5() -> None:
    """Process a payment."""
    ws_search_key_C5 = item_account_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag_C5 == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance_C5 -= item_amount_C5
        update_account_2350_C5()
        ws_payment_count_C5 += 1

def process_refund_3270_C5() -> None:
    """Process a refund."""
    ws_search_key_C5 = item_account_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag_C5 == 'Y':
        ws_account_balance_C5 += item_amount_C5
        update_account_2350_C5()
        ws_refund_count_C5 += 1

def process_adjustment_3280_C5() -> None:
    """Process an adjustment."""
    ws_search_key_C5 = item_account_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if ws_found_flag_C5 == 'Y':
        if item_amount_C5 > 0:
            ws_account_balance_C5 += item_amount_C5
        else:
            ws_account_balance_C5 -= item_amount_C5
        update_account_2350_C5()
        ws_adjustment_count_C5 += 1

def validate_batch_totals_3300_C5() -> None:
    """Validate batch totals."""
    if ws_actual_count_C5 != ws_expected_count_C5:
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'BATCH COUNT MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()
    if ws_actual_total_C5 != ws_expected_total_C5:
        ws_error_msg_C5 = 'BATCH TOTAL MISMATCH'
        reject_batch_3350_C5()

def reject_batch_3350_C5() -> None:
    """Reject the batch."""
    ws_rejection_record_C5 = WsRejectionRecordC5()
    ws_rejection_record_C5.REJ_BATCH_ID = ws_current_batch_C5
    ws_rejection_record_C5.REJ_REASON = ws_error_msg_C5
    ws_rejection_record_C5.REJ_DATE = current_date_function_C5()
    write_rejection_record_C5(ws_rejection_record_C5)
    ws_rejected_batch_count_C5 += 1

def commit_batch_3400_C5() -> None:
    """Commit the batch."""
    if ws_batch_valid_C5 == 'Y':
        ws_committed_batch_count_C5 += 1
        update_batch_status_3450_C5()

def update_batch_status_3450_C5() -> None:
    """Update batch status."""

    batch_header_record_C5.BATCH_STATUS = 'COMMITTED'
    batch_header_record_C5.BATCH_COMMIT_DATE = current_date_function_C5()
    rewrite_batch_header_record_C5(batch_header_record_C5)

    ws_exception_idx_C5 = 1
    while ws_exception_idx_C5 <= ws_error_count_C5:

def calculate_simple_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate simple interest."""
    global WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6 = (
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 * WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6 / 36500
    )

def calculate_compound_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate compound interest."""
    global WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6, WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 = (1 + WS_INTEREST_RATE_C6 / 36500) ** WS_DAYS_IN_PERIOD_C6
    WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6 = WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 * (WS_COMPOUND_FACTOR_C6 - 1)

def apply_interest_C6() -> None:
    """Apply interest."""
    global WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6
    if WS_INTEREST_METHOD_C6 == "S":
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_SIMPLE_INTEREST_C6
    else:
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 += WS_COMPOUND_INTEREST_C6
    update_account_C6()

def fee_processing_C6() -> None:
    """Fee processing."""
    calculate_monthly_fee_C6()
    calculate_transaction_fees_C6()
    apply_fee_waivers_C6()
    deduct_fees_C6()

def calculate_monthly_fee_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate monthly fee."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == "CHK":
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 12.00
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == "SAV":
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 5.00
    elif WS_ACCOUNT_TYPE_C6 == "PRM":
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 25.00
    else:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 0.00

def calculate_transaction_fees_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate transaction fees."""
    global WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    if WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 > WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6:
        WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 = WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6 - WS_FREE_TRANS_LIMIT_C6
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_EXCESS_TRANS_C6 * WS_PER_TRANS_FEE_C6
    else:
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = 0

def apply_fee_waivers_C6() -> None:
    """Apply fee waivers."""
    global WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6, WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 >= WS_MIN_BALANCE_WAIVER_C6:
        WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 = 0
    if WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == "GOLD" or WS_CUSTOMER_TIER_C6 == "PLATINUM":
        WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 = WS_TRANS_FEE_C6 * 0.5

def deduct_fees_C6() -> None:
    """Deduct fees."""
    global WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6
    WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6 = WS_MONTHLY_FEE_C6 + WS_TRANS_FEE_C6
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C6 -= None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    update_account_C6()
    record_fee_transaction_C6()

def record_fee_transaction_C6() -> None:
    """Record fee transaction."""
    global FEE_ACCOUNT_C6, FEE_AMOUNT_C6, FEE_DESCRIPTION_C6, FEE_DATE_C6
    FEE_ACCOUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C6
    FEE_AMOUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_FEES_C6
    FEE_DESCRIPTION_C6 = "MONTHLY FEE"
    # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO fee_date - placeholder
    FEE_DATE_C6 = "CURRENT DATE"
    # WRITE fee_record FROM ws_fee_record - placeholder
    pass

def finalization_C6() -> None:
    """Finalization."""
    write_control_totals_C6()
    close_files_C6()
    display_summary_C6()

def write_control_totals_C6() -> None:
    """Write control totals."""
    global CTL_TRANS_COUNT_C6, CTL_DEPOSITS_C6, CTL_WITHDRAWALS_C6, CTL_ERROR_COUNT_C6, CTL_RUN_DATE_C6
    CTL_TRANS_COUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6
    CTL_DEPOSITS_C6 = WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS_C6
    CTL_WITHDRAWALS_C6 = WS_TOTAL_WITHDRAWALS_C6
    CTL_ERROR_COUNT_C6  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_COUNT_C6
    # MOVE FUNCTION current_date TO ctl_run_date - placeholder
    CTL_RUN_DATE_C6 = "CURRENT DATE"
    # WRITE control_record FROM ws_control_record - placeholder
    pass

def close_files_C6() -> None:
    """Close files."""
    # CLOSE customer_file account_file transaction_file report_file error_file master_file - placeholder
    pass

def display_summary_C6() -> None:
    """Display summary."""
    print("==========================================")
    print("mega_enterprise PROCESSING COMPLETE")
    print("==========================================")
    print("TRANSACTIONS PROCESSED: ", WS_TRANS_COUNT_C6)
    print("DEPOSITS:              ", WS_DEPOSIT_COUNT_C6)
    print("WITHDRAWALS:           ", WS_WITHDRAWAL_COUNT_C6)
    print("TRANSFERS:             ", WS_TRANSFER_COUNT_C6)
    print("ERRORS:                ", WS_ERROR_COUNT_C6)
    print("TOTAL DEPOSITS:   $", WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS_C6)

def abort_process_C6() -> None:
    """Abort process."""
    print("CRITICAL ERROR: ", WS_ABORT_REASON_C6)
    print("PROCESSING ABORTED AT ", "CURRENT DATE") #  FUNCTION current_date placeholder
    close_files_C6()
    exit(8)

def loan_processing_C6() -> None:
    """Loan processing."""
    validate_loan_application_C6()
    if WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 == "Y":
        calculate_credit_score_C6()
        assess_risk_C6()
        determine_approval_C6()
        if WS_RISK_ASSESSMENT_AREA_C6.approval_status == "A":
            generate_loan_terms_C6()
            create_amortization_C6()
            finalize_loan_C6()
        else:
            process_decline_C6()

def validate_loan_application_C6() -> None:
    """Validate loan application."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C6, WS_ERROR_MSG_C6
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'Y'
    if WS_LOAN_PROCESSING_AREA_C6.loan_amount < 1000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'MINIMUM LOAN AMOUNT IS $1000'
        return None
    if WS_LOAN_PROCESSING_AREA_C6.loan_amount > 10000000:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'MAXIMUM LOAN AMOUNT EXCEEDED'
        return None
    if WS_LOAN_PROCESSING_AREA_C6.loan_term_months < 6 or WS_LOAN_PROCESSING_AREA_C6.loan_term_months > 360:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C6 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C6 = 'INVALID LOAN TERM'

def calculate_credit_score_C6() -> None:
    """Calculate credit score."""
    WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_score = 0
    score_payment_history_C6()
    score_credit_utilization_C6()
    score_credit_length_C6()
    score_new_credit_C6()
    score_credit_mix_C6()
    determine_tier_C6()

def score_payment_history_C6() -> None:
    """Score payment history."""
    global WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6
    WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = (
        (WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.payment_history.on_time_payments * 100) / 0  # TODO
        (WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.payment_history.on_time_payments + WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.payment_history.late_30_days + 0  # TODO
         WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.payment_history.late_60_days + WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.payment_history.late_90_days)
    )
    WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 = WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6 * 0.35
    WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_score += None  # TODO: was WS_PAYMENT_SCORE_C6

def score_credit_utilization_C6() -> None:
    """Score credit utilization."""
    global WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6
    if WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_utilization <= 10:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = 100
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_utilization <= 30:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = 80
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_utilization <= 50:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = 60
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_utilization <= 75:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = 40
    else:
        WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = 20
    WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 = WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6 * 0.30
    WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_score += None  # TODO: was WS_UTIL_SCORE_C6

def score_credit_length_C6() -> None:
    """Score credit length."""
    global WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6
    if WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_history_len >= 84:
        WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 = 100
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_history_len >= 60:
        WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 = 80
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_history_len >= 36:
        WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 = 60
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_history_len >= 12:
        WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 = 40
    else:
        WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 = 20
    WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 = WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6 * 0.15
    WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_score += None  # TODO: was WS_LENGTH_SCORE_C6

def score_new_credit_C6() -> None:
    """Score new credit."""
    global WS_NEW_SCORE_C6
    if WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.new_credit_inqs == 0:
        WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 = 100
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.new_credit_inqs <= 2:
        WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 = 80
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.new_credit_inqs <= 4:
        WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 = 60
    elif WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.new_credit_inqs <= 6:
        WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 = 40
    else:
        WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 = 20
    WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 = WS_NEW_SCORE_C6 * 0.10
    WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_C6.credit_score += None  # TODO: was WS_NEW_SCORE_C6

def score_credit_mix_C6() -> None:
    """Score credit mix."""
    global WS_MIX_SCORE_C6
    if WS_CREDIT_SCORING_AREA_

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates PMI amount based on LTV ratio."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates loan history and adjusts risk score."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Calculates final risk score and category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return int(ws_risk_score), ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: Decimal, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status and conditions."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    ws_approved_rate = calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier, ws_base_rate, ws_risk_category)
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.00")
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("0.50")
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("1.50")
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal("3.00")
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal("0.50")
    return ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[int], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal]]:
    """Creates amortization schedule."""
    amort_interest: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_principal: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_balance: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_payment_num: List[int] = []
    amort_payment_amt: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_escrow: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_total_pmt: List[Decimal] = []

    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    ws_payment_date = "00000000" # Current date
    ws_payment_month = 1 # Current month
    ws_payment_year = 2024 # Current year
    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        (interest, principal, balance, payment_num, payment_amt, escrow, total_pmt, ws_running_balance, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year) = calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance, ws_monthly_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, loan_mortgage, ws_property_tax, ws_insurance_premium, ws_pmi_amount, ws_amort_idx, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year)

        amort_interest.append(interest)
        amort_principal.append(principal)
        amort_balance.append(balance)
        amort_payment_num.append(payment_num)
        amort_payment_amt.append(payment_amt)
        amort_escrow.append(escrow)
        amort_total_pmt.append(total_pmt)

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, ws_amort_idx: int, ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, int, int]:
    """Calculates payment split for amortization."""
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num = ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt

    if loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_escrow = Decimal("0.00")
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year = advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year, ws_amort_idx)

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_escrow, amort_total_pmt, ws_running_balance, ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int, ws_amort_idx: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Advances payment date by one month."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    amort_payment_date = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1

    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def finalize_loan_C7(ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_id: str, ws_loan_type: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_interest_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal) -> None:
    """Finalizes loan processing."""
    ws_loan_start_date = "00000000" # Current date
    ws_loan_end_date = ws_loan_start_date + (ws_loan_term_months * 30)
    ws_loan_status = 'A'
    create_loan_record_C7(ws_loan_id, ws_loan_type, ws_loan_amount, ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_loan_monthly_pmt, ws_loan_start_date, ws_loan_status)
    disburse_funds_C7(ws_loan_amount)
    send_confirmation_C7()

    loan_rec_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate
    loan_rec_payment = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    loan_rec_start = ws_loan_start_date
    loan_rec_status = ws_loan_status

    # WRITE loan_record FROM ws_loan_record
    pass

def portfolio_management_C7() -> None:
    """Manages investment portfolio."""
    load_portfolio_C7()
    update_market_prices_C7()
    calculate_values_C7()
    rebalance_check_C7()
    generate_statements_C7()

def load_portfolio_C7() -> tuple[List[Any], int]:
    """Loads portfolio from file."""
    ws_holding: List[Any] = []
    ws_hold_idx = 1
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_hold_idx <= 100 and ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        # READ holdings_file INTO ws_holding_rec
        ws_holding_rec = ""
        if True: # AT END
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else: # NOT AT END
            ws_holding.append(ws_holding_rec)
            ws_hold_idx += 1
    ws_holdings_count = ws_hold_idx - 1
    return ws_holding, ws_holdings_count

def update_market_prices_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_symbol: List[str], hold_current_price: List[Decimal]) -> List[Decimal]:
    """Updates market prices for holdings."""
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        ws_quote_symbol = hold_symbol[ws_hold_idx - 1]
        ws_quote_price = get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol)
        hold_current_price.append(ws_quote_price)
    return hold_current_price

def get_quote_C7(ws_quote_symbol: str) -> Decimal:
    """Gets quote for a symbol."""
    quote_request_symbol = ws_quote_symbol
    # CALL 'GETQUOTE' USING quote_request quote_response
    quote_response_status = "OK" # Dummy response
    quote_last_price = Decimal("100.00") # Dummy price
    if quote_response_status == 'OK':
        ws_quote_price = quote_last_price
    else:
        ws_quote_price = Decimal("0.00")
    return ws_quote_price

def calculate_values_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_cost_per_share: List[Decimal], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], hold_gain_loss: List[Decimal], hold_pct_change: List[Decimal]) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates values for portfolio holdings."""
    ws_total_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cost_basis = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_unrealized_gain = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        (market_value, gain_loss, pct_change, ws_cost) = calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx, hold_shares, hold_current_price, hold_cost_per_share)
        hold_market_value.append(market_value)
        hold_gain_loss.append(gain_loss)
        hold_pct_change.append(pct_change)
        ws_total_value += market_value
        ws_cost_basis += ws_cost
        ws_unrealized_gain += gain_loss
    return hold_market_value, hold_gain_loss, hold_pct_change, ws_total_value, ws_cost_basis, ws_unrealized_gain

def calculate_holding_value_C7(ws_hold_idx: int, hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_cost_per_share: List[Decimal]) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates holding value."""
    hold_market_value = Decimal(hold_shares[ws_hold_idx - 1]) * hold_current_price[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    ws_hold_cost = Decimal(hold_shares[ws_hold_idx - 1]) * hold_cost_per_share[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    hold_gain_loss = hold_market_value - ws_hold_cost
    if ws_hold_cost > 0:
        hold_pct_change = (hold_gain_loss / ws_hold_cost) * 100
    else:
        hold_pct_change = Decimal("0.00")
    return hold_market_value, hold_gain_loss, hold_pct_change, ws_hold_cost

def rebalance_check_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_holdings_count: int, hold_type: List[str], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> None:
    """Checks if rebalancing is needed."""
    ws_rebalance_needed: str = "N"
    ws_stocks_diff: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bonds_diff: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")
    (ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct) = calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_holdings_count, hold_type, hold_market_value, ws_total_value)
    (ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_diff, ws_bonds_diff) = compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_target_stocks_pct, ws_target_bonds_pct)
    if ws_rebalance_needed == 'Y':
        generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value, ws_stocks_diff)

def calculate_current_allocation_C7(ws_holdings_count: int, hold_type: List[str], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], ws_total_value: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates current asset allocation."""
    ws_stocks_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_bonds_value = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_cash_value = Decimal("0.00")
    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        if hold_type[ws_hold_idx - 1] == 'STK':
            ws_stocks_value += hold_market_value[ws_hold_idx - 1]
        elif hold_type[ws_hold_idx - 1] == 'BND':
            ws_bonds_value += hold_market_value[ws_hold_idx - 1]
        elif hold_type[ws_hold_idx - 1] == 'CSH':
            ws_cash_value += hold_market_value[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    ws_stocks_pct = (ws_stocks_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_bonds_pct = (ws_bonds_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    ws_cash_pct = (ws_cash_value / ws_total_value) * 100
    return ws_stocks_pct, ws_bonds_pct, ws_cash_pct

def compare_to_target_C7(ws_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_bonds_pct: Decimal, ws_target_stocks_pct: Decimal, ws_target_bonds_pct: Decimal) -> tuple[str, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Compares current allocation to target allocation."""
    ws_rebalance_needed = 'N'
    ws_stocks_diff = ws_stocks_pct - ws_target_stocks_pct
    ws_bonds_diff = ws_bonds_pct - ws_target_bonds_pct
    if abs(ws_stocks_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    if abs(ws_bonds_diff) > 5:
        ws_rebalance_needed = 'Y'
    return ws_rebalance_needed, ws_stocks_diff, ws_bonds_diff

def generate_rebalance_trades_C7(ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_stocks_diff: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generates rebalance trades."""
    if ws_stocks_diff > 0:
        ws_sell_amount = ws_total_value * ws_stocks_diff / 100
        create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount)
    else:
        ws_buy_amount = ws_total_value * (0 - ws_stocks_diff) / 100
        create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount)

def create_sell_order_C7(ws_sell_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Creates a sell order."""
    ws_trade_type = 'SELL'
    ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount = ws_sell_amount
    trade_execution_C7()

def create_buy_order_C7(ws_buy_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Creates a buy order."""
    ws_trade_type = 'BUY '
    ws_order_type = 'MARKET'
    ws_trade_amount = ws_buy_amount
    trade_execution_C7()

def generate_statements_C7(ws_end_of_quarter: str, ws_end_of_year: str, ws_total_value: Decimal, ws_quarter_start_value: Decimal, ws_dividend_income: Decimal, ws_realized_gain_ytd: Decimal, hold_symbol: List[str], hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], hold_gain_loss: List[Decimal], ws_holdings_count: int) -> None:
    """Generates investment statements."""
    monthly_statement_C7(hold_symbol, hold_shares, hold_current_price, hold_market_value, hold_gain_loss, ws_holdings_count)

        quarterly_report_C7(ws_total_value, ws_quarter_start_value)
    if ws_end_of_year == 'Y':
        annual_tax_report_C7(ws_dividend_income, ws_realized_gain_ytd)

def monthly_statement_C7(hold_symbol: List[str], hold_shares: List[int], hold_current_price: List[Decimal], hold_market_value: List[Decimal], hold_gain_loss: List[Decimal], ws_holdings_count: int) -> None:
