def trade_execution_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a trade."""
    validate_order_C7()
    ws_order_valid = "Y"
    if ws_order_valid == 'Y':
        ws_available_cash = Decimal("10000")
        ws_trade_shares = 10
        ws_estimated_price = Decimal("100")
        ws_current_shares = 10
        ws_trade_symbol = "AAPL"
        (ws_sufficient_flag) = check_funds_shares_C7(ws_available_cash, ws_trade_shares, ws_estimated_price, ws_current_shares, ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_sufficient_flag == 'Y':
            route_order_C7()
            execute_order_C7()
            settle_trade_C7()
        else:
            reject_order_C7()

def validate_order_C7() -> None:
    """Validates a trade order."""
    ws_order_valid = 'Y'
    ws_trade_symbol = ""
    ws_trade_shares = 0
    ws_limit_price = Decimal("0")
    ws_reject_reason = ""
    if ws_trade_symbol == '':
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'SYMBOL REQUIRED'
        return None
    if ws_trade_shares <= 0:
        ws_order_valid = 'N'
        ws_reject_reason = 'INVALID QUANTITY'
        return None
    order_limit = False
    order_stop_limit = False
    if order_limit or order_stop_limit:
        if ws_limit_price <= 0:
            ws_order_valid = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'LIMIT PRICE REQUIRED'

def check_funds_shares_C7(ws_available_cash: Decimal, ws_trade_shares: int, ws_estimated_price: Decimal, ws_current_shares: int, ws_trade_symbol: str) -> str:
    """Checks if there are sufficient funds or shares."""
    ws_sufficient_flag = 'Y'
    ws_reject_reason = ""
    trade_buy = True
    trade_sell = False
    if trade_buy:
        ws_required_funds = ws_trade_shares * ws_estimated_price
        if ws_required_funds > ws_available_cash:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if trade_sell:
        (ws_current_shares) = check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol)
        if ws_current_shares < ws_trade_shares:
            ws_sufficient_flag = 'N'
            ws_reject_reason = 'INSUFFICIENT SHARES'
    return ws_sufficient_flag

def check_share_position_C7(ws_trade_symbol: str) -> int:
    """Checks the current share position."""
    ws_current_shares = 0
    ws_holding: List[Any] = []
    ws_holdings_count = 0
    hold_symbol: List[str] = []

    for ws_hold_idx in range(1, ws_holdings_count + 1):
        if hold_symbol[ws_hold_idx - 1] == ws_trade_symbol:
            hold_shares: List[int] = []
            ws_current_shares += hold_shares[ws_hold_idx - 1]
    return ws_current_shares

def route_order_C7() -> None:
    """Routes the order to the appropriate exchange."""
    ws_routing_type = ""
    ws_trade_amount = Decimal("0")
    if ws_trade_amount > 100000:
        ws_routing_type = 'ALGO'
    elif ws_trade_amount > 10000:
        ws_routing_type = 'SMART'
    else:
        ws_routing_type = 'DIRECT'
    ws_order_time = "00000000" # Current date

def execute_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes the trade order."""
    order_market = True
    order_limit = False
    order_stop = False
    order_stop_limit = False

    if order_market:
        market_order_C7()
    elif order_limit:
        limit_order_C7()
    elif order_stop:
        stop_order_C7()
    else:
        stop_limit_order_C7()

def market_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a market order."""
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("100")
    ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
    ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
    ws_execution_time = "00000000" # Current date

def limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a limit order."""
    ws_trade_status = ""
    trade_buy = True
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("100")
    ws_limit_price = Decimal("90")
    if trade_buy:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'
    else:
        if ws_current_market_price >= ws_limit_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a stop order."""
    ws_trade_status = ""
    trade_sell = True
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("100")
    ws_stop_price = Decimal("90")
    if trade_sell:
        if ws_current_market_price <= ws_stop_price:
            ws_executed_price = ws_current_market_price
            ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
        else:
            ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def stop_limit_order_C7() -> None:
    """Executes a stop-limit order."""
    ws_current_market_price = Decimal("100")
    ws_stop_price = Decimal("90")
    ws_trade_status = ""
    if ws_current_market_price <= ws_stop_price:
        limit_order_C7()
    else:
        ws_trade_status = 'OPEN'

def settle_trade_C7() -> None:
    """Settles a trade."""
    ws_trade_status = 'FILLED'
    if ws_

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12

def underwriting_C8() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8()
    check_medical_history_C8()
    verify_information_C8()
    determine_decision_C8()

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluate risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if False:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Check medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5

def verify_information_C8() -> None:
    """Verify information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8()
    validate_documents_C8()

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str) -> None:
    """Check fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validate documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determine decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str) -> None:
    """Issue policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8()
        create_policy_record_C8()
        set_beneficiaries_C8()
        send_policy_docs_C8()
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8()

def generate_policy_number_C8(ws_policy_type: str, ws_policy_number: str, ws_date_part: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: int) -> None:
    """Generate policy number."""
    import datetime
    ws_date_part = str(datetime.date.today())
    ws_type_part = ws_policy_type
    import random
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_policy_number = ws_type_part + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)

def create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str) -> None:
    """Create policy record."""
    policy_rec_number = ws_policy_number
    policy_rec_type = ws_policy_type
    policy_rec_coverage = ws_coverage_amount
    policy_rec_premium = ws_annual_premium
    policy_rec_eff_date = ws_effective_date
    policy_rec_exp_date = ws_expiration_date
    policy_rec_status = 'A'
    # WRITE policy_record FROM ws_policy_record
    pass

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number: str, benef_name: list[str], benef_relation: list[str], benef_pct: list[int]) -> None:
    """Set beneficiaries."""
    for ws_benef_idx in range(1, 6):
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx - 1].strip() != "":
            benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx - 1]
            # WRITE beneficiary_record FROM ws_beneficiary_rec
            pass

def send_policy_docs_C8(ws_policy_number: str) -> None:
    """Send policy documents."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_issue'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Your policy ' + ws_policy_number + ' has been issued'
    send_notification_C8()

def send_decline_letter_C8() -> None:
    """Send decline letter."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_decline'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification_C8()

def claims_handling_C8() -> None:
    """Handle claims."""
    receive_claim_C8()
    validate_claim_C8()
    investigate_claim_C8()
    adjudicate_claim_C8()
    process_payment_C8()

def receive_claim_C8() -> None:
    """Receive claim."""
    import datetime
    ws_claim_date = str(datetime.date.today())
    generate_claim_number_C8()
    ws_claim_status = 'RECEIVED'

def generate_claim_number_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_date_part: str, ws_random_part: int) -> None:
    """Generate claim number."""
    import datetime
    ws_date_part = str(datetime.date.today())
    import random
    ws_random_part = int(random.random() * 99999)
    ws_claim_number = 'CLM' + ws_date_part + str(ws_random_part)

def validate_claim_C8() -> None:
    """Validate claim."""
    check_policy_status_C8()
    check_coverage_C8()
    check_deductible_C8()

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Check policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Check coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Check deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Investigate claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8()
    fraud_check_C8(ws_coverage_amount, ws_recent_claims, ws_claim_amount)

def assign_adjuster_C8() -> None:
    """Assign adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'

def fraud_check_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Fraud check."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.8'):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicate claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'

def process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status: str) -> None:
    """Process payment."""
    if ws_claim_status == 'APPROVED':
        issue_payment_C8()
        update_claim_record_C8()

def issue_payment_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Issue payment."""
    import datetime
    pay_rec_claim = ws_claim_number
    pay_rec_amount = ws_approved_amount
    pay_rec_date = str(datetime.date.today())
    pay_rec_method = 'CHECK'
    # WRITE payment_record FROM ws_payment_record
    pass

def update_claim_record_C8() -> None:
    """Update claim record."""
    import datetime
    ws_claim_status = 'PAID'
    ws_claim_close_date = str(datetime.date.today())
    # REWRITE claim_record
    pass

def payroll_processing_C8(ws_employee_id: str) -> None:
    """Process payroll."""
    load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id)
    calculate_gross_pay_C8()
    calculate_taxes_C8()
    calculate_deductions_C8()
    calculate_net_pay_C8()
    generate_paystubs_C8()
    process_direct_deposit_C8()

def load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id: str) -> None:
        """TODO"""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8()
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8()

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = Decimal('40') * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - Decimal('40')
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal('1.5')
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay

def calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate commission pay."""
    ws_base_pay = ws_base_salary / ws_pay_periods
    ws_commission_pay = ws_sales_amount * ws_commission_rate
    ws_gross_pay = ws_base_pay + ws_commission_pay

def calculate_taxes_C8() -> None:
    """Calculate taxes."""
    calc_federal_tax_C8()
    calc_state_tax_C8()
    calc_local_tax_C8()
    calc_fica_C8()

def calc_federal_tax_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_exemptions: int, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_annualized_gross: Decimal, ws_allowance_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate federal tax."""
    ws_annualized_gross = ws_gross_pay * ws_pay_periods
    ws_allowance_amount = ws_exemptions * 4300
    ws_taxable_income = ws_annualized_gross - ws_allowance_amount
    if ws_taxable_income < 0:
        ws_taxable_income = Decimal('0')
    apply_tax_brackets_C8()
    ws_federal_tax = ws_annual_tax / ws_pay_periods

def apply_tax_brackets_C8(status_single: bool, status_married_joint: bool, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Apply tax brackets."""
    ws_annual_tax = Decimal('0')
    if status_single:
        single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)
    elif status_married_joint:
        married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax)

def single_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate single tax brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 10275:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 41775:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('1027.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 10275) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 89075:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('4807.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 41775) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 170050:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('15213.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 89075) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 215950:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('34647.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 170050) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 539900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('49335.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 215950) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('162718.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 539900) * Decimal('0.37')

def married_brackets_C8(ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate married tax brackets."""
    if ws_taxable_income <= 20550:
        ws_annual_tax = ws_taxable_income * Decimal('0.10')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 83550:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('2055.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 20550) * Decimal('0.12')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 178150:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('9615.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 83550) * Decimal('0.22')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 340100:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('30427.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 178150) * Decimal('0.24')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 431900:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('69295.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 340100) * Decimal('0.32')
    elif ws_taxable_income <= 647850:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('98671.00') + (ws_taxable_income - 431900) * Decimal('0.35')
    else:
        ws_annual_tax = Decimal('174253.50') + (ws_taxable_income - 647850) * Decimal('0.37')

def calc_state_tax_C8(ws_state_code: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate state tax."""
    if ws_state_code == 'CA':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0725')
    elif ws_state_code == 'NY':
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0685')
    elif ws_state_code == 'TX':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    elif ws_state_code == 'FL':
        ws_state_tax = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_state_tax = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.05')

def calc_local_tax_C8(ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate local tax."""
    if ws_local_tax_rate > 0:
        ws_local_tax = ws_gross_pay * ws_local_tax_rate
    else:
        ws_local_tax = Decimal('0')

def calc_fica_C8(ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_additional_medicare: Decimal, ws_remaining_cap: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate FICA."""
    if ws_ytd_gross < 160200:
        ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - ws_ytd_gross
        if ws_gross_pay <= ws_remaining_cap:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.062')
        else:
            ws_fica_ss = ws_remaining_cap * Decimal('0.062')
    else:
        ws_fica_ss = Decimal('0')
    ws_fica_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.0145')
    if ws_ytd_gross > 200000:
        ws_additional_medicare = ws_gross_pay * Decimal('0.009')
        ws_fica_medicare += ws_additional_medicare

def calculate_deductions_C8() -> None:
    """Calculate deductions."""
    calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8()
    calc_post_tax_deductions_C8()

def calc_pre_tax_deductions_C8(ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate pre-tax deductions."""
    if ws_401k_pct > 0:
        ws_401k_contrib = ws_gross_pay * ws_401k_pct / 100
        if ws_ytd_401k + ws_401k_contrib > 22500:
            ws_401k_contrib = 22500 - ws_ytd_401k
            if ws_401k_contrib < 0:
                ws_401k_contrib = Decimal('0')
    ws_health_ins = ws_health_ins_deduct
    ws_dental_ins = ws_dental_ins_deduct
    ws_vision_ins = ws_vision_ins_deduct
    ws_hsa_contrib = ws_hsa_deduct
    ws_fsa_contrib = ws_fsa_deduct

def calc_post_tax_deductions_C8(ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishment_amt: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate post-tax deductions."""
    ws_life_ins = ws_life_ins_deduct
    ws_disability_ins = ws_disability_deduct
    ws_union_dues = ws_union_dues_amt
    ws_garnishment = ws_garnishment_amt

def calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishment: Decimal, ws_other_deduct: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_total_deductions: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate net pay."""
    ws_total_deductions = (
        ws_federal_tax
        + ws_state_tax
        + ws_local_tax
        + ws_fica_ss
        + ws_fica_medicare
        + ws_health_ins
        + ws_dental_ins
        + ws_vision_ins
        + ws_401k_contrib
        + ws_hsa_contrib
        + ws_fsa_contrib
        + ws_life_ins
        + ws_disability_ins
        + ws_union_dues
        + ws_garnishment
        + ws_other_deduct
    )
    ws_net_pay = ws_gross_pay - ws_total_deductions
    update_ytd_totals_C8()

def update_ytd_totals_C8(ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_ytd_fed_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_state_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_fica: Decimal, ws_ytd_net: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal) -> None:
    """Update year-to-date totals."""
    ws_ytd_gross += ws_gross_pay
    ws_ytd_fed_tax += ws_federal_tax
    ws_ytd_state_tax += ws_state_tax
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_ss
    ws_ytd_fica += ws_fica_medicare
    ws_ytd_net += ws_net_pay
    ws_ytd_401k += ws_401k_contrib

def generate_paystubs_C8(ws_employee_id: str, ws_pay_period: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_ytd_net: Decimal) -> None:

    stub_ytd_net = ws_ytd_net
    # WRITE paystub_record FROM ws_paystub_record

def validate_bank_info_C8(ws_routing_number: str, ws_account_number: str, ws_dd_valid: str) -> None:
    """Validate bank info."""
    if ws_routing_number.strip() == "":
        ws_dd_valid = 'N'
    elif ws_account_number.strip() == "":
        ws_dd_valid = 'N'
    else:
        ws_dd_valid = 'Y'

        # WRITE ach_record FROM ws_ach_record
        pass

def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: Any, schedule_type: str) -> None:
    """Calculates the next run date based on schedule type."""
    if schedule_type == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 1
    elif schedule_type == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 7
    elif schedule_type == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 30
    elif schedule_type == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 90
    elif schedule_type == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_last_run_date) + 365
    else:
        pass

def function_integer_of_date_C10(date: Any) -> int:
    """Placeholder for date to integer conversion."""
    pass

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global ws_total_trans_amount, ws_total_trans_count, ws_avg_trans_amount, ws_eof_flag
    ws_total_trans_amount = 0
    ws_total_trans_count = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount = 0

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = read_transaction_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'
            ws_total_trans_count += 1
            ws_total_trans_amount += ws_trans_rec.trans_amount
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

@dataclass
class TransactionRecordC10:
    trans_amount: Decimal

def read_transaction_file_C10() -> TransactionRecordC10:
    """Placeholder to read transaction file."""
    pass

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects customer metrics."""
    global ws_active_customers, ws_new_customers, ws_churned_customers, ws_eof_flag, ws_period_start

    ws_active_customers = 0
    ws_new_customers = 0
    ws_churned_customers = 0

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = read_customer_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'

            if ws_cust_rec.cust_status == 'A':
                ws_active_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_open_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_new_customers += 1
            if ws_cust_rec.cust_close_date >= ws_period_start:
                ws_churned_customers += 1

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

@dataclass
class CustomerRecordC10:
    cust_status: str
    cust_open_date: Any
    cust_close_date: Any

def read_customer_file_C10() -> CustomerRecordC10:
    """Placeholder to read customer file."""
    pass

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects performance metrics."""
    global ws_response_time_total, ws_response_count, ws_avg_response_time, ws_eof_flag
    ws_response_time_total = 0
    ws_response_count = 0

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = read_perf_log_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'
            ws_response_time_total += ws_perf_rec.perf_response_time
            ws_response_count += 1
        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs weekly aggregation."""
    global ws_day_of_week, ws_week_number
    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummaryRecordC10(
            weekly_week=ws_week_number,
            weekly_trans_count=0,
            weekly_trans_amount=0
        )
        sum_week_data_C10(ws_weekly_summary)
        write_weekly_summary_record_C10(ws_weekly_summary)

    weekly_week: int
    weekly_trans_count: int
    weekly_trans_amount: Decimal

    """Placeholder to write weekly summary record."""
    pass

def sum_week_data_C10(weekly_summary: WeeklySummaryRecordC10) -> None:
    """Sums weekly data."""
    global daily_trans_count, daily_trans_amount
    weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count = 0
    weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount = 0

    for _ in range(7):
        weekly_summary.weekly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
        weekly_summary.weekly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs monthly aggregation."""
    global ws_end_of_month, ws_curr_month, ws_curr_year
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummaryRecordC10(
            monthly_month=ws_curr_month,
            monthly_year=ws_curr_year,
            monthly_trans_count=0,
            monthly_trans_amount=0,
            monthly_new_accounts=0,
            monthly_closed_accounts=0
        )
        sum_month_data_C10(ws_monthly_summary)
        write_monthly_summary_record_C10(ws_monthly_summary)

    pass

def sum_month_data_C10(monthly_summary: MonthlySummaryRecordC10) -> None:
    """Sums monthly data."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_curr_month
    monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count = 0
    monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount = 0
    monthly_summary.monthly_new_accounts = 0
    monthly_summary.monthly_closed_accounts = 0

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'

            if ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_month == ws_curr_month:
                monthly_summary.monthly_trans_count += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count
                monthly_summary.monthly_trans_amount += ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates KPIs."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    global ws_total_assets, ws_net_income, ws_roa, ws_total_equity, ws_roe, ws_interest_expense, ws_nim, ws_interest_income, ws_earning_assets
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100

    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100

    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    global ws_total_trans_count, ws_error_count, ws_error_rate, ws_sla_compliance, ws_within_sla_count, ws_total_cases, ws_first_call_resolution, ws_fcr_count, ws_total_calls
    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100

    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    global ws_active_customers, ws_churned_customers, ws_churn_rate, ws_acquisition_cost, ws_marketing_spend, ws_new_customers, ws_lifetime_value, ws_avg_revenue_per_customer, ws_avg_customer_tenure
    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100

    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

    create_executive_dashboard_C10()

def create_executive_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates executive dashboard."""
    global dash_title, dash_revenue, dash_net_income, dash_roa, dash_roe, dash_customers, ws_total_revenue, ws_net_income, ws_roa, ws_roe, ws_active_customers
    dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    dash_revenue = ws_total_revenue
    dash_net_income = ws_net_income
    dash_roa = ws_roa
    dash_roe = ws_roe
    dash_customers = ws_active_customers

    ws_exec_dashboard = ExecutiveDashboardRecordC10(
        dash_title=dash_title,
        dash_revenue=dash_revenue,
        dash_net_income=dash_net_income,
        dash_roa=dash_roa,
        dash_roe=dash_roe,
        dash_customers=dash_customers
    )
    write_dashboard_record_C10(ws_exec_dashboard)

def create_risk_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates risk dashboard."""
    global dash_title, dash_fraud_score, dash_npl, dash_capital, dash_liquidity, ws_fraud_score, ws_npl_ratio, ws_capital_ratio, ws_liquidity_ratio
    dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD'
    dash_fraud_score = ws_fraud_score
    dash_npl = ws_npl_ratio
    dash_capital = ws_capital_ratio
    dash_liquidity = ws_liquidity_ratio

    ws_risk_dashboard = RiskDashboardRecordC10(
        dash_title=dash_title,
        dash_fraud_score=dash_fraud_score,
        dash_npl=dash_npl,
        dash_capital=dash_capital,
        dash_liquidity=dash_liquidity
    )
    write_dashboard_record_C10(ws_risk_dashboard)

@dataclass
class RiskDashboardRecordC10:
    dash_title: str
    dash_fraud_score: int
    dash_npl: Decimal
    dash_capital: Decimal
    dash_liquidity: Decimal

def export_data_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data."""
    export_csv_C10()
    export_xml_C10()
    export_json_C10()

def export_csv_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to CSV."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_csv_header, ws_csv_line
    open_output_csv_export_file_C10()
    ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
    write_csv_record_C10(ws_csv_header)

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'

            ws_csv_line = f"{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_date},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_deposits},{ws_daily_sum_rec.daily_withdrawals}"
            write_csv_record_C10(ws_csv_line)

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    close_csv_export_file_C10()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    """Placeholder to open CSV export file."""
    pass

def export_xml_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to XML."""
    global ws_xml_line
    open_output_xml_export_file_C10()
    ws_xml_line = '<?xml version="1.0"?>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)
    ws_xml_line = '<DailySummaries>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)
    write_xml_records_C10()
    ws_xml_line = '</DailySummaries>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)
    close_xml_export_file_C10()

def write_xml_records_C10() -> None:
    """Writes XML records."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'
            format_xml_record_C10(ws_daily_sum_rec)

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record_C10(daily_sum_rec: DailySummaryFileRecordC10) -> None:
    """Formats XML record."""
    global ws_xml_line
    ws_xml_line = '<Summary>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)

    ws_xml_line = f'<Date>{daily_sum_rec.daily_date}</Date>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)

    ws_xml_line = f'<TransCount>{daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count}</TransCount>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)

    ws_xml_line = '</Summary>'
    write_xml_record_C10(ws_xml_line)

def export_json_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to JSON."""
    global ws_json_line
    open_output_json_export_file_C10()
    ws_json_line = '{"dailySummaries":['
    write_json_record_C10(ws_json_line)
    write_json_records_C10()
    ws_json_line = ']}'
    write_json_record_C10(ws_json_line)
    close_json_export_file_C10()

def write_json_records_C10() -> None:
    """Writes JSON records."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_first_record
    ws_first_record = 'N'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = read_daily_summary_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'
            format_json_record_C10(ws_daily_sum_rec)

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record_C10(daily_sum_rec: DailySummaryFileRecordC10) -> None:
    """Formats JSON record."""
    global ws_json_comma, ws_first_record, ws_json_line
    if ws_first_record == 'Y':
        ws_json_comma = ','
    else:
        ws_json_comma = ''
        ws_first_record = 'Y'

    ws_json_line = f'{ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{daily_sum_rec.daily_date}","transCount":{daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_count},"transAmount":{daily_sum_rec.daily_trans_amount}}}'
    write_json_record_C10(ws_json_line)

def account_maintenance_C10() -> None:
    """Account maintenance procedures."""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()

def dormant_account_check_C10() -> None:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = read_account_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'
            check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec)

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

@dataclass
class AccountRecordC10:
    acct_last_activity: Any
    acct_status: str
    acct_status_desc: str
    acct_dormant_date: Any
    acct_balance: Decimal
    acct_id: str
    acct_owner_name: str
    acct_owner_address: str
    acct_pending_trans: int
    acct_loan_link: str

def read_account_file_C10() -> AccountRecordC10:
    """Placeholder to read account file."""
    pass

def check_activity_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Checks account activity."""
    global ws_process_date
    ws_days_inactive = function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date) - function_integer_of_date_C10(account_rec.acct_last_activity)
    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        account_rec.acct_status = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10(account_rec)

def mark_dormant_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Marks account as dormant."""
    global ws_process_date
    account_rec.acct_status_desc = 'DORMANT'
    account_rec.acct_dormant_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_account_record_C10(account_rec)
    send_dormant_notice_C10(account_rec)

def rewrite_account_record_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Placeholder to rewrite account record."""
    pass

def send_dormant_notice_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Sends dormant account notice."""
    global ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject
    ws_notif_type = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()

def send_notification_C10() -> None:
    """Placeholder to send notification."""
    pass

def escheatment_processing_C10() -> None:
    """Processes escheatment."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = read_account_file_C10()
            ws_eof_flag = 'N'
            if ws_account_rec.acct_status == 'D':
                check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec)

        except EOFError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_escheatment_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Checks for escheatment."""
    global ws_process_date, ws_escheat_years
    ws_dormant_years = (function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date) - function_integer_of_date_C10(account_rec.acct_dormant_date)) / 365
    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account_C10(account_rec)

def escheat_account_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Escheats the account."""
    global ws_escheat_amount
    account_rec.acct_status = 'E'
    ws_escheat_amount = account_rec.acct_balance
    account_rec.acct_balance = 0
    create_escheat_record_C10(account_rec)
    rewrite_account_record_C10(account_rec)

def create_escheat_record_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Creates escheat record."""
    global ws_process_date, ws_escheat_amount
    ws_escheat_record = EscheatRecordC10(
        escheat_account=account_rec.acct_id,
        escheat_amount=ws_escheat_amount,
        escheat_date=ws_process_date,
        escheat_owner=account_rec.acct_owner_name,
        escheat_address=account_rec.acct_owner_address
    )
    write_escheat_record_C10(ws_escheat_record)

@dataclass
class EscheatRecordC10:
    escheat_account: str
    escheat_amount: Decimal
    escheat_date: Any
    escheat_owner: str
    escheat_address: str

def write_escheat_record_C10(escheat_record: EscheatRecordC10) -> None:
    """Placeholder to write escheat record."""
    pass

def account_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account closure."""
    global ws_close_request
    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        ws_account_rec = AccountRecordC10("", "", "", "", Decimal(0), "", "", "", 0, "")
        validate_closure_C10(ws_account_rec)
        if ws_closure_valid == 'Y':
            process_closure_C10(ws_account_rec)
        else:
            reject_closure_C10()

def validate_closure_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Validates account closure."""
    global ws_closure_valid, ws_closure_reject
    ws_closure_valid = 'Y'
    if account_rec.acct_balance < 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'
    if account_rec.acct_pending_trans > 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'
    if account_rec.acct_loan_link != '':
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'

def process_closure_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Processes account closure."""
    global ws_final_balance, ws_process_date
    ws_final_balance = account_rec.acct_balance
    disburse_balance_C10(account_rec)
    account_rec.acct_status = 'C'
    account_rec.acct_close_date = ws_process_date
    rewrite_account_record_C10(account_rec)
    archive_account_C10(account_rec)

def disburse_balance_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Disburses account balance."""
    global ws_final_balance
    if ws_final_balance > 0:
        ws_check_record = CheckRecordC10(
            check_from_account=account_rec.acct_id,
            check_amount=ws_final_balance,
            check_memo='ACCOUNT CLOSURE',
            check_payee=account_rec.acct_owner_name
        )
        write_check_record_C10(ws_check_record)

@dataclass
class CheckRecordC10:
    check_from_account: str
    check_amount: Decimal
    check_memo: str
    check_payee: str

def write_check_record_C10(check_record: CheckRecordC10) -> None:
    """Placeholder to write check record."""
    pass

def archive_account_C10(account_rec: AccountRecordC10) -> None:
    """Archives the account."""
    global ws_process_date
    ws_archive_record = ArchiveRecordC10(
        archive_account_data=account_rec,
        archive_date=ws_process_date,
        archive_retention=function_integer_of_date_C10(ws_process_date) + 2555
    )
    write_archive_record_C10(ws_archive_record)

@dataclass
class ArchiveRecordC10:
    archive_account_data: AccountRecordC10
    archive_date: Any
    archive_retention: int

def write_archive_record_C10(archive_record: ArchiveRecordC10) -> None:
    """Placeholder to write archive record."""
    pass

def reject_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Rejects account closure."""
    global ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject, ws_closure_reject
    ws_notif_type = 'closure_reject'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Closure rejected'

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Blocks a card."""
    global CARD_STATUS, WS_BLOCK_REASON, CARD_BLOCK_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CARD_BLOCK_DATE, CARD_RECORD, WS_CARD_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_BODY

    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    # Assuming REWRITE card_record updates the CARD_RECORD based on WS_CARD_RECORD
    # Need actual rewrite logic here; this is a placeholder
    CARD_RECORD  = None  # TODO: was WS_CARD_RECORD
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = f'Your card has been blocked: {WS_BLOCK_REASON}'
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Executes wire transfer procedures."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validates a wire transfer request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED

    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if not WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """Screens a wire transfer request against OFAC."""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK

    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE)
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE)
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def ofacsrch_C11(ofac_request: Optional[OfacRequestC11], ofac_response: Optional[OfacResponseC11]) -> None:
    """Placeholder for OFAC search function."""
    pass

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Processes a wire transfer."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debits the originator's account."""

    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Transmits a wire message."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE, SWIFT_STATUS, WS_WIRE_STATUS

    swiftsend_C11(WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE)
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:

    process_ach_credits_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """Receives an ACH file."""
    global ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER, ACH_FILE_ID, WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, ACH_CREATION_DATE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, ACH_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES

    #OPEN INPUT ach_input_file (Assuming ACH_INPUT_FILE is a file object)
    #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_file_header
    WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER = WsAchFileHeaderC11()  # Initialize from read
    ACH_FILE_ID = "some_ach_file_id" # Example, replace with actual logic
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID
    ACH_CREATION_DATE = 20240101 # Example, replace with actual logic
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_CREATION_DATE
    ACH_ENTRY_COUNT = 100 # Example, replace with actual logic
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Validates ACH entries."""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG

    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_ACH_ENTRY = WsAchEntryC11()  #Read into WS_ACH_ENTRY
            validate_single_entry_C11()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Validates a single ACH entry."""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, ACH_ROUTING, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES

    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not ACH_ROUTING.isnumeric():
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if not ACH_ACCOUNT:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """Processes ACH credits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_TRANS_CODE

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_ACH_ENTRY = WsAchEntryC11() # Read ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('22', '23', '32', '33'):
                apply_credit_C11()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """Applies an ACH credit."""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE

    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def search_account_C11() -> None:
    """Placeholder for searching an account."""
    pass

    """Creates a return entry."""

    WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY = WsAchReturnEntryC11()  # Initialize
    RETURN_ORIG_TRACE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_TRACE_NUMBER
    RETURN_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    RETURN_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """Processes ACH debits."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_TRANS_CODE

    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_ACH_ENTRY = WsAchEntryC11() #READ ach_input_file INTO ws_ach_entry
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('27', '28', '37', '38'):
                apply_debit_C11()
        except EOFError:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Applies an ACH debit."""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE

    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generates an ACH return file."""
    global WS_RETURN_COUNT

    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_file_C11() -> None:
    """Creates an ACH return file."""
    #OPEN OUTPUT ach_return_file
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    #CLOSE ach_return_file
    pass

def write_return_header_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the return file header."""

    WS_RETURN_HEADER = WsReturnHeaderC11()  # Initialize
    RETURN_RECORD_TYPE = '1'
    RETURN_PRIORITY_CODE = '01'
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_DEST  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_ROUTING
    RETURN_IMMEDIATE_ORIGIN  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    RETURN_FILE_DATE = 20240101 # Example, replace with date logic
    ach_return_record_write_C11(WS_RETURN_HEADER)

def write_return_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Writes the return entries."""
    global WS_RETURN_IDX, WS_RETURN_COUNT

    WS_RETURN_IDX = 1  # Initialize index
    while WS_RETURN_IDX <= WS_RETURN_COUNT:
        # Assuming WS_RETURN_ENTRY is a list
        ach_return_record_write_C11(WsAchReturnEntryC11()) #WS_RETURN_ENTRY[WS_RETURN_IDX - 1])  # 1-based indexing
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

def write_return_trailer_C11() -> None:

def validate_stop_request_C12() -> None:
    """Validates the stop request."""
    global ws_stop_valid_C12, ws_check_number_C12, ws_stop_reject_C12, ws_check_already_cleared_C12
    ws_stop_valid_C12 = 'Y'
    if ws_check_number_C12 == 0:
        ws_stop_valid_C12 = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject_C12 = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if ws_check_already_cleared_C12 == 'Y':
        ws_stop_valid_C12 = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject_C12 = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def create_stop_order_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a stop order."""
    global ws_stop_record_C12, acct_id_C12, stop_account_C12, ws_check_number_C12, stop_check_number_C12
    global ws_check_amount_C12, stop_amount_C12, ws_payee_name_C12, stop_payee_C12, ws_process_date_C12
    global stop_effective_date_C12, stop_expiry_date_C12, stop_status_C12, stop_record_C12
    ws_stop_record_C12 = WsStopRecordC12()
    stop_account_C12 = acct_id_C12
    stop_check_number_C12 = ws_check_number_C12
    stop_amount_C12 = ws_check_amount_C12
    stop_payee_C12 = ws_payee_name_C12
    stop_effective_date_C12 = ws_process_date_C12
    stop_expiry_date_C12 = int(ws_process_date_C12) + 180
    stop_status_C12 = 'A'
    stop_record_C12 = StopRecordC12()

def apply_stop_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Applies the stop fee."""
    global ws_stop_payment_fee_C12, ws_account_balance_C12, ws_notif_type_C12, ws_notif_channel_C12
    global ws_check_number_C12, ws_notif_subject_C12
    ws_account_balance_C12 -= ws_stop_payment_fee_C12
    update_account_C12()
    ws_notif_type_C12 = 'stop_payment'
    ws_notif_channel_C12 = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject_C12 = f'Stop payment placed on check #{ws_check_number_C12}'
    send_notification_C12()

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """Handles safe deposit box procedures."""
    box_rental_C12()
    box_access_C12()
    box_drilling_C12()
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box rental."""
    global ws_rental_request_C12
    if ws_rental_request_C12 == 'Y':
        check_availability_C12()
        global ws_box_available_C12
        if ws_box_available_C12 == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12() -> None:
    """Checks box availability."""
    global ws_box_available_C12, ws_box_idx_C12, ws_total_boxes_C12, box_status_C12, box_size_C12
    global ws_requested_size_C12, ws_assigned_box_C12
    ws_box_available_C12 = 'N'
    ws_box_idx_C12 = 1
    while ws_box_idx_C12 <= ws_total_boxes_C12:
        if box_status_C12[ws_box_idx_C12] == 'A':
            if box_size_C12[ws_box_idx_C12] == ws_requested_size_C12:
                ws_box_available_C12 = 'Y'
                ws_assigned_box_C12 = ws_box_idx_C12
                break
        ws_box_idx_C12 += 1

def assign_box_C12() -> None:
    """Assigns a box."""
    global ws_assigned_box_C12, box_status_C12, box_renter_C12, ws_customer_id_C12, box_rental_date_C12, ws_process_date_C12
    box_status_C12[ws_assigned_box_C12] = 'R'
    box_renter_C12[ws_assigned_box_C12] = ws_customer_id_C12
    box_rental_date_C12[ws_assigned_box_C12] = ws_process_date_C12

def create_rental_agreement_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a rental agreement."""
    global ws_rental_agreement_C12, ws_assigned_box_C12, rental_box_number_C12, ws_customer_id_C12
    global rental_customer_C12, ws_process_date_C12, rental_start_date_C12, rental_annual_fee_C12
    global ws_box_size_fee_C12, ws_requested_size_C12, rental_record_C12
    ws_rental_agreement_C12 = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    rental_box_number_C12 = ws_assigned_box_C12
    rental_customer_C12 = ws_customer_id_C12
    rental_start_date_C12 = ws_process_date_C12
    rental_annual_fee_C12 = ws_box_size_fee_C12[ws_requested_size_C12]
    rental_record_C12 = RentalRecordC12()

def box_access_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box access."""
    global ws_access_request_C12
    if ws_access_request_C12 == 'Y':
        verify_renter_C12()
        global ws_renter_verified_C12
        if ws_renter_verified_C12 == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Verifies the renter."""
    global ws_renter_verified_C12, box_renter_C12, ws_box_number_C12, ws_customer_id_C12, ws_id_verified_C12, ws_key_verified_C12
    ws_renter_verified_C12 = 'N'
    if box_renter_C12[ws_box_number_C12] == ws_customer_id_C12:
        if ws_id_verified_C12 == 'Y':
            if ws_key_verified_C12 == 'Y':
                ws_renter_verified_C12 = 'Y'

def log_access_C12() -> None:
    """Logs the access."""
    global ws_access_log_C12, ws_box_number_C12, access_box_number_C12, ws_customer_id_C12, access_customer_C12
    global ws_process_date_C12, access_date_C12, access_time_C12, access_type_C12, access_log_record_C12
    ws_access_log_C12 = WsAccessLogC12()
    access_box_number_C12 = ws_box_number_C12
    access_customer_C12 = ws_customer_id_C12
    access_date_C12 = ws_process_date_C12
    access_time_C12 = "CURRENT_TIME" # FUNCTION current_time
    access_type_C12 = 'ENTRY'
    access_log_record_C12 = AccessLogRecordC12()

def escort_to_vault_C12() -> None:
    """Escorts to the vault."""
    global ws_display_msg_C12
    ws_display_msg_C12 = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'

def box_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box drilling."""
    global ws_drilling_request_C12
    if ws_drilling_request_C12 == 'Y':
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        global ws_drilling_authorized_C12
        if ws_drilling_authorized_C12 == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Validates drilling authorization."""
    global ws_drilling_authorized_C12, ws_rent_delinquent_months_C12, ws_court_order_C12, ws_deceased_renter_C12, ws_executor_verified_C12
    ws_drilling_authorized_C12 = 'N'
    if ws_rent_delinquent_months_C12 >= 12:
        ws_drilling_authorized_C12 = 'Y'
    if ws_court_order_C12 == 'Y':
        ws_drilling_authorized_C12 = 'Y'
    if ws_deceased_renter_C12 == 'Y':
        if ws_executor_verified_C12 == 'Y':
            ws_drilling_authorized_C12 = 'Y'

def schedule_drilling_C12() -> None:
    """Schedules drilling."""
    global ws_drilling_record_C12, ws_box_number_C12, drill_box_number_C12, ws_drilling_reason_C12, drill_reason_C12
    global ws_process_date_C12, drill_scheduled_date_C12, drilling_record_C12
    ws_drilling_record_C12 = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    drill_box_number_C12 = ws_box_number_C12
    drill_reason_C12 = ws_drilling_reason_C12
    drill_scheduled_date_C12 = int(ws_process_date_C12) + 30
    drilling_record_C12 = DrillingRecordC12()

def notify_renter_C12() -> None:
    """Notifies the renter."""
    global ws_notif_type_C12, ws_notif_channel_C12, ws_notif_subject_C12
    ws_notif_type_C12 = 'box_drilling'
    ws_notif_channel_C12 = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject_C12 = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12()

def box_billing_C12() -> None:
    """Handles box billing."""
    global ws_box_idx_C12, ws_total_boxes_C12, box_status_C12, box_renewal_due_C12
    ws_box_idx_C12 = 1
    while ws_box_idx_C12 <= ws_total_boxes_C12:
        if box_status_C12[ws_box_idx_C12] == 'R':
            if box_renewal_due_C12[ws_box_idx_C12] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12()
        ws_box_idx_C12 += 1

def charge_annual_fee_C12() -> None:
    """Charges the annual fee."""
    global ws_box_idx_C12, box_renter_C12, ws_customer_id_C12, box_annual_fee_C12, ws_fee_amount_C12, ws_account_balance_C12, box_next_renewal_C12
    ws_customer_id_C12 = box_renter_C12[ws_box_idx_C12]
    ws_fee_amount_C12 = box_annual_fee_C12[ws_box_idx_C12]
    ws_account_balance_C12 -= ws_fee_amount_C12
    update_account_C12()
    box_next_renewal_C12[ws_box_idx_C12] = box_next_renewal_C12[ws_box_idx_C12] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """Handles merchant services procedures."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """Processes authorization."""
    validate_card_C12()
    global ws_card_valid_C12
    if ws_card_valid_C12 == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        global ws_fraud_approved_C12
        if ws_fraud_approved_C12 == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            global ws_credit_available_C12
            if ws_credit_available_C12 == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12() -> None:
    """Validates the card."""
    global ws_card_valid_C12
    ws_card_valid_C12 = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    global ws_luhn_valid_C12
    if ws_luhn_valid_C12 == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        global ws_not_expired_C12
        if ws_not_expired_C12 == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            global ws_cvv_valid_C12
            if ws_cvv_valid_C12 == 'Y':
                ws_card_valid_C12 = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the Luhn algorithm."""
    global ws_luhn_sum_C12, ws_luhn_idx_C12, ws_auth_card_number_C12, ws_luhn_digit_C12, ws_luhn_valid_C12
    ws_luhn_sum_C12 = 0
    ws_luhn_idx_C12 = 16
    while ws_luhn_idx_C12 >= 1:
        ws_luhn_digit_C12 = int(ws_auth_card_number_C12[ws_luhn_idx_C12 - 1])
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx_C12) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit_C12 *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit_C12 > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit_C12 -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum_C12 += ws_luhn_digit_C12
        ws_luhn_idx_C12 -= 1
    if ws_luhn_sum_C12 % 10 == 0:
        ws_luhn_valid_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_luhn_valid_C12 = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the expiry date."""
    global ws_auth_expiry_date_C12, ws_process_date_C12, ws_not_expired_C12
    if ws_auth_expiry_date_C12 >= ws_process_date_C12:
        ws_not_expired_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_not_expired_C12 = 'N'

def check_cvv_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the CVV."""
    global ws_auth_card_number_C12, ws_auth_cvv_C12, ws_cvv_result_C12, ws_cvv_valid_C12
    cvvverify(ws_auth_card_number_C12, ws_auth_cvv_C12, ws_cvv_result_C12)
    if ws_cvv_result_C12 == 'M':
        ws_cvv_valid_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_cvv_valid_C12 = 'N'

def check_fraud_score_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the fraud score."""
    global ws_auth_request_C12, ws_fraud_response_C12, fraud_score_C12, ws_fraud_approved_C12, fraud_decline_code_C12, ws_auth_decline_code_C12
    fraudcheck(ws_auth_request_C12, ws_fraud_response_C12)
    if fraud_score_C12 < 70:
        ws_fraud_approved_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_fraud_approved_C12 = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code_C12 = fraud_decline_code_C12

def check_available_credit_C12() -> None:
    """Checks the available credit."""
    global ws_auth_card_number_C12, ws_search_key_C12, card_account_file_C12, ws_card_account_rec_C12, ws_available_credit_C12, ws_auth_amount_C12, ws_credit_available_C12, ws_auth_decline_code_C12
    ws_search_key_C12 = ws_auth_card_number_C12
    ws_card_account_rec_C12 = CardAccountFileC12()
    if ws_available_credit_C12 >= ws_auth_amount_C12:
        ws_credit_available_C12 = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_credit_available_C12 = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code_C12 = '51'

def approve_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Approves authorization."""
    global ws_auth_response_code_C12, ws_auth_amount_C12, ws_available_credit_C12
    ws_auth_response_code_C12 = '00'
    generate_auth_code_C12()
    ws_available_credit_C12 -= ws_auth_amount_C12
    record_authorization_C12()

def generate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """Generates authorization code."""
    global ws_auth_code_C12, ws_auth_response_auth_code_C12
    ws_auth_code_C12 = "RANDOM" # FUNCTION RANDOM
    ws_auth_code_C12 *= 999999
    ws_auth_response_auth_code_C12 = ws_auth_code_C12

def record_authorization_C12() -> None:
    """Records authorization."""
    global ws_auth_record_C12, ws_auth_card_number_C12, auth_rec_card_C12, ws_auth_amount_C12, auth_rec_amount_C12, ws_auth_response_auth_code_C12, auth_rec_code_C12, ws_process_date_C12, auth_rec_date_C12, auth_rec_time_C12, ws_merchant_id_C12, auth_rec_merchant_C12, auth_rec_status_C12, auth_record_C12
    ws_auth_record_C12 = WsAuthRecordC12()
    auth_rec_card_C12 = ws_auth_card_number_C12
    auth_rec_amount_C12 = ws_auth_amount_C12
    auth_rec_code_C12 = ws_auth_response_auth_code_C12
    auth_rec_date_C12 = ws_process_date_C12
    auth_rec_time_C12 = "CURRENT_TIME" # FUNCTION current_time
    auth_rec_merchant_C12 = ws_merchant_id_C12
    auth_rec_status_C12 = 'P'
    auth_record_C12 = AuthRecordC12()

def decline_auth_C12() -> None:
    """Declines authorization."""
    global ws_auth_decline_code_C12, ws_auth_response_code_C12, ws_decline_record_C12, ws_auth_card_number_C12, decline_rec_card_C12, ws_auth_amount_C12, decline_rec_amount_C12, decline_rec_code_C12, ws_process_date_C12, decline_rec_date_C12, decline_record_C12
    ws_auth_response_code_C12 = ws_auth_decline_code_C12
    ws_decline_record_C12 = WsDeclineRecordC12()
    decline_rec_card_C12 = ws_auth_card_number_C12
    decline_rec_amount_C12 = ws_auth_amount_C12
    decline_rec_code_C12 = ws_auth_decline_code_C12
    decline_rec_date_C12 = ws_process_date_C12
    decline_record_C12 = DeclineRecordC12()

def capture_transaction_C12() -> None:
    """Captures a transaction."""
    global ws_capture_request_C12
    if ws_capture_request_C12 == 'Y':
        validate_auth_code_C12()
        global ws_auth_valid_C12
        if ws_auth_valid_C12 == 'Y':
            create_capture_record_C12()

def validate_auth_code_C12() -> None:
    """Validates authorization code."""
    global ws_auth_valid_C12, ws_capture_auth_code_C12, auth_search_key_C12, auth_file_C12, ws_auth_rec_C12, auth_rec_status_C12
    ws_auth_valid_C12 = 'N'
    auth_search_key_C12 = ws_capture_auth_code_C12
    ws_auth_rec_C12 = WsAuthRecC12()

def create_capture_record_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a capture record."""
    global auth_rec_status_C12, ws_auth_rec_C12, ws_capture_record_C12, auth_rec_card_C12, capture_card_C12, ws_capture_amount_C12, capture_amount_C12, ws_capture_auth_code_C12, capture_auth_code_C12, ws_process_date_C12, capture_date_C12, capture_record_C12
    auth_rec_status_C12 = 'C'
    ws_capture_record_C12 = WsCaptureRecordC12()
    capture_card_C12 = auth_rec_card_C12
    capture_amount_C12 = ws_capture_amount_C12
    capture_auth_code_C12 = ws_capture_auth_code_C12
    capture_date_C12 = ws_process_date_C12
    capture_record_C12 = CaptureRecordC12()

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """Processes settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()
    send_settlement_file_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12() -> None:
    """Batches transactions."""
    global ws_batch_total_C12, ws_batch_count_C12, ws_eof_flag_C12, capture_file_C12, ws_capture_rec_C12, capture_amount_C12, capture_settled_C12
    ws_batch_total_C12 = 0
    ws_batch_count_C12 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C12 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C12 == 'N':
        ws_capture_rec_C12 = WsCaptureRecC12()
        if ws_eof_flag_C12 == 'Y':
            ws_eof_flag_C12 = 'Y'
        else:
            if capture_settled_C12 == 'N':
                ws_batch_total_C12 += capture_amount_C12
                ws_batch_count_C12 += 1
                capture_settled_C12 = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag_C12 = 'N'

def calculate_fees_C12() -> None:
    """Calculates fees."""
    global ws_batch_total_C12, ws_interchange_fee_C12, ws_assessment_fee_C12, ws_batch_count_C12, ws_processor_fee_C12, ws_total_fees_C12
    ws_interchange_fee_C12 = ws_batch_total_C12 * 0.0175
    ws_assessment_fee_C12 = ws_batch_total_C12 * 0.0015
    ws_processor_fee_C12 = ws_batch_count_C12 * 0.10
    ws_total_fees_C12 = ws_interchange_fee_C12 + ws_assessment_fee_C12 + ws_processor_fee_C12

def create_funding_record_C12() -> None:
    """Creates a funding record."""
    global ws_batch_total_C12, ws_total_fees_C12, ws_net_funding_C12, ws_funding_record_C12, ws_merchant_id_C12, funding_merchant_C12, funding_amount_C12, funding_fees_C12, ws_process_date_C12, funding_date_C12, funding_record_C12
    ws_net_funding_C12 = ws_batch_total_C
