def logging_utilities_C13() -> None:
    """Calls logging functions."""
    log_info_C13()
    log_warning_C13()
    log_error_C13()

def log_info_C13() -> None:
    """Logs info message."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'INFO'
    LOG_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_LOG_MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_LOG_ENTRY = "" # Assuming assignment is needed for WRITE operation

def log_warning_C13() -> None:
    """Logs warning message."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'WARN'
    LOG_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_LOG_MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_LOG_ENTRY = "" # Assuming assignment is needed for WRITE operation

def log_error_C13() -> None:
    """Logs error message."""
    global LOG_LEVEL, WS_LOG_MESSAGE, LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_LOG_ENTRY
    LOG_LEVEL = 'ERROR'
    LOG_MESSAGE  = None  # TODO: was WS_LOG_MESSAGE
    LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_LOG_ENTRY = "" # Assuming assignment is needed for WRITE operation

def error_handling_C13() -> None:
    """Calls error handling functions."""
    format_error_C13()
    display_error_C13()
    write_error_log_C13()

def format_error_C13() -> None:
    """Formats the error message."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR, WS_ERROR_CODE, WS_ERROR_MSG
    WS_FORMATTED_ERROR = f"ERROR: {WS_ERROR_CODE} - {WS_ERROR_MSG}"

def display_error_C13() -> None:
    """Displays the formatted error message."""
    global WS_FORMATTED_ERROR
    print(WS_FORMATTED_ERROR)

def write_error_log_C13() -> None:
    """Writes error to error log."""
    global WS_ERROR_LOG_REC, ERR_LOG_CODE, ERR_LOG_MSG, ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP, WS_PROGRAM_NAME, WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME
    WS_ERROR_LOG_REC = "" # Initialize
    ERR_LOG_CODE  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_CODE
    ERR_LOG_MSG  = None  # TODO: was WS_ERROR_MSG
    ERR_LOG_TIMESTAMP = datetime.now()
    WS_PROGRAM_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROGRAM_NAME
    WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_PARAGRAPH_NAME

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:
    """Manages treasury functions."""
    calculate_cash_position_C13()
    project_cash_flows_C13()
    manage_reserves_C13()
    manage_investments_C13()
    manage_borrowings_C13()

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates cash position."""
    global WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_CASH_POSITION = Decimal('0.00')
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums vault cash."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_VAULT_REC
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_VAULT_REC = "" # READ vault_cash_file INTO
            VAULT_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # from the read
            WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was VAULT_BALANCE
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums federal account."""
    global WS_FED_BALANCE, WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_FED_BALANCE = Decimal('0.00')  #  READ fed_account_file
    WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was WS_FED_BALANCE

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums correspondent balances."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CASH_POSITION, WS_CORR_REC
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_CORR_REC = "" # READ correspondent_file INTO
            CORR_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # from the read
            WS_CASH_POSITION += None  # TODO: was CORR_BALANCE
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects cash flows."""
    global WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS, WS_NET_POSITION, WS_CASH_POSITION
    WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS = Decimal('0.00')
    WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS = Decimal('0.00')
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    WS_NET_POSITION = WS_CASH_POSITION + WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS - WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects loan payments."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, WS_LOAN_PMT_REC, LOAN_PMT_DATE, LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT, WS_PROJECTION_DATE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_LOAN_PMT_REC = "" # READ loan_schedule_file
            LOAN_PMT_DATE: int = 0 # Date from the read
            LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # Amount from the read
            if LOAN_PMT_DATE <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS += None  # TODO: was LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects deposit flows."""
    global WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS, WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS, WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS, WS_PROJECTION_DAYS, WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS, WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS
    WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS = WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS = WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS += WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS
    WS_PROJECTED_OUTFLOWS += WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS

def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Projects investment maturities."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS, WS_INV_REC, INV_MATURITY_DATE, INV_PAR_VALUE, WS_PROJECTION_DATE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        try:
            WS_INV_REC = "" # READ investment_file
            INV_MATURITY_DATE: int = 0 # Maturity date from the read
            INV_PAR_VALUE: Decimal = Decimal('0.00') # Par value from the read
            if INV_MATURITY_DATE <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                WS_PROJECTED_INFLOWS += None  # TODO: was INV_PAR_VALUE
        except:
            WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manages reserves."""
    global WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    if WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()

def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates reserve requirement."""
    global WS_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT, WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_RESERVE_RATIO
    WS_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT = WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS * WS_RESERVE_RATIO

def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Checks reserve position."""
    global WS_EXCESS_RESERVES, WS_FED_BALANCE, WS_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT, WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY
    WS_EXCESS_RESERVES = WS_FED_BALANCE - WS_RESERVE_REQUIREMENT
    if WS_EXCESS_RESERVES < 0:
        WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY = 'N'

def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Covers reserve shortfall."""
    global WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT, WS_EXCESS_RESERVES
    WS_SHORT

def main_logic_C15() -> None:
    """Main program logic."""
    global WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL_C15, WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL_C15, WS_RECON_DIFF_C15
    WS_RECON_DIFF_C15 = WS_GL_CONTROL_BAL_C15 - WS_SUBLEDGER_TOTAL_C15
    if WS_RECON_DIFF_C15 != 0:
        log_recon_exception_37235_C15()

def log_recon_exception_37235_C15() -> None:
    """Logs a reconciliation exception."""
    global WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_C15, WS_GL_ACCOUNT_C15, RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT_C15, WS_RECON_DIFF_C15, RECON_EXC_DIFF_C15, RECON_EXC_DATE_C15
    WS_RECON_EXCEPTION_C15 = ReconExceptionRecordC15(account="", diff=Decimal("0"), date="")
    RECON_EXC_ACCOUNT_C15  = None  # TODO: was WS_GL_ACCOUNT_C15
    RECON_EXC_DIFF_C15  = None  # TODO: was WS_RECON_DIFF_C15
    RECON_EXC_DATE_C15 = str(datetime.now())
    # WRITE recon_exception_record FROM ws_recon_exception
    pass

def intercompany_recon_37300_C15() -> None:
    """Performs intercompany reconciliation."""
    load_ic_balances_37310_C15()
    match_ic_pairs_37320_C15()
    report_ic_differences_37330_C15()

def load_ic_balances_37310_C15() -> None:
    """Loads intercompany balances from file."""
    global WS_IC_COUNT_C15, WS_EOF_FLAG_C15, WS_IC_BALANCE_C15, WS_IC_ARRAY_C15
    WS_IC_COUNT_C15 = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 != 'Y':
        # READ intercompany_file INTO ws_ic_balance
        WS_IC_BALANCE_C15 = None
        if True: # AT END (simulated)
            WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 = 'Y'
        else: # NOT AT END
            WS_IC_COUNT_C15 += 1
            WS_IC_ARRAY_C15.append(WS_IC_BALANCE_C15)
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_37320_C15() -> None:
    """Matches intercompany balance pairs."""
    global WS_IC_IDX_C15, WS_IC_COUNT_C15
    WS_IC_IDX_C15 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX_C15 <= WS_IC_COUNT_C15:
        find_ic_counterpart_37325_C15()
        WS_IC_IDX_C15 += 1

def find_ic_counterpart_37325_C15() -> None:
    """Finds the counterpart for an intercompany balance."""
    global IC_FROM_ENTITY_C15, IC_TO_ENTITY_C15, WS_SEARCH_FROM_C15, WS_SEARCH_TO_C15, WS_IC_IDX2_C15, WS_IC_COUNT_C15, WS_IC_DIFF_C15
    WS_SEARCH_FROM_C15  = None  # TODO: was IC_FROM_ENTITY_C15
    WS_SEARCH_TO_C15  = None  # TODO: was IC_TO_ENTITY_C15
    WS_IC_IDX2_C15 = 1
    while WS_IC_IDX2_C15 <= WS_IC_COUNT_C15:
        if IC_FROM_ENTITY_C15 == WS_SEARCH_TO_C15:
            if IC_TO_ENTITY_C15 == WS_SEARCH_FROM_C15:
                WS_IC_DIFF_C15 = IC_FROM_ENTITY_C15 + IC_TO_ENTITY_C15
                if WS_IC_DIFF_C15 != 0:
                    log_ic_diff_37326_C15()
                break
        WS_IC_IDX2_C15 += 1

def log_ic_diff_37326_C15() -> None:
    """Logs an intercompany difference."""
    global WS_IC_DIFF_REC_C15, WS_SEARCH_FROM_C15, ICD_FROM_C15, WS_SEARCH_TO_C15, ICD_TO_C15, WS_IC_DIFF_C15, ICD_AMOUNT_C15
    WS_IC_DIFF_REC_C15 = IcDiffRecC15(from_entity="", to_entity="", amount=Decimal("0"))
    ICD_FROM_C15  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_FROM_C15
    ICD_TO_C15  = None  # TODO: was WS_SEARCH_TO_C15
    ICD_AMOUNT_C15  = None  # TODO: was WS_IC_DIFF_C15
    # WRITE ic_diff_record FROM ws_ic_diff_rec
    pass

def move_to_archive_38410_C15() -> None:
    """Moves audit logs to the archive."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG_C15, WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15, WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP_C15, WS_ARCHIVE_DATE_C15
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 != 'Y':
        # READ audit_file INTO ws_audit_record
        WS_AUDIT_RECORD_C15 = None
        if True: # AT END (simulated)
            WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 = 'Y'
        else: # NOT AT END
            if WS_AUDIT_TIMESTAMP_C15 < WS_ARCHIVE_DATE_C15:
                # WRITE archive_audit_record FROM ws_audit_record
                # DELETE audit_file
                pass
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C15 = 'N'

def cpu_metrics_39110_C15() -> None:
    """Collects CPU metrics."""
    global WS_CPU_UTILIZATION_C15, WS_CPU_ALERT_C15
    WS_CPU_UTILIZATION_C15 = get_cpu_utilization()
    if WS_CPU_UTILIZATION_C15 > 80:
        WS_CPU_ALERT_C15 = 'Y'

def memory_metrics_39120_C15() -> None:
    """Collects memory metrics."""
    global WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION_C15, WS_MEMORY_ALERT_C15
    WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION_C15 = get_memory_utilization()
    if WS_MEMORY_UTILIZATION_C15 > 85:
        WS_MEMORY_ALERT_C15 = 'Y'

def io_metrics_39130_C15() -> None:
    """Collects I/O metrics."""
    global WS_IO_WAIT_TIME_C15, WS_IO_THRESHOLD_C15, WS_IO_ALERT_C15
    WS_IO_WAIT_TIME_C15 = get_io_wait_time()
    if WS_IO_WAIT_TIME_C15 > WS_IO_THRESHOLD_C15:
        WS_IO_ALERT_C15 = 'Y'

def transaction_metrics_39140_C15() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global WS_TPS_C15, WS_TRANS_COUNT_C15, WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS_C15, WS_AVG_RESPONSE_C15, WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME_C15
    if WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS_C15 != 0:
        WS_TPS_C15 = WS_TRANS_COUNT_C15 / WS_ELAPSED_SECONDS_C15
    else:
        WS_TPS_C15 = 0

    if WS_TRANS_COUNT_C15 != 0:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_C15 = WS_TOTAL_RESPONSE_TIME_C15 / WS_TRANS_COUNT_C15
    else:
        WS_AVG_RESPONSE_C15 = 0

def analyze_performance_39200_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes performance metrics."""
    global WS_AVG_RESPONSE_C15, WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD_C15, WS_PERF_DEGRADED_C15, WS_TPS_C15, WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD_C15, WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW_C15
    if WS_AVG_RESPONSE_C15 > WS_RESPONSE_THRESHOLD_C15:
        WS_PERF_DEGRADED_C15 = 'Y'
    if WS_TPS_C15 < WS_MIN_TPS_THRESHOLD_C15:
        WS_THROUGHPUT_LOW_C15 = 'Y'
